原油だけでなく石油精製品や天然ガスなどにも供給制約。インフレ圧力は長期化
◼ 原油高に加え、製油能力逼迫に伴うクラック・スプレッドの上昇もグローバルなインフレを助長する懸念
― クラック・スプレッド（石油精製品と原油の価格差）は例年を大きく上回る水準。戦争の影響を背景に中東・ロシアからの
輸出が減少していること等が影響
◼ 代替ルートの乏しい天然ガスや肥料（尿素など）の供給懸念は継続。先物価格は高止まり
― 天然ガスでは中東依存度が高い欧州やアジアで価格が上昇。欧州ガス価格（TTF）は前年比で約2.4倍まで急騰

― 先物価格も需給ひっ迫継続を見越して高止まり。中東情勢次第では、天然ガス価格がさらに上振れる可能性も

◼ 電力・人材需要の増加
◼ 資金需要の拡大

インフレ長期化：エネルギー供給ショックや投資需要がインフレ圧力に
◼ ホルムズ海峡の封鎖長期化により、エネルギー価格は当面高止まり
― 開戦以降、グローバルCPIのエネルギー指数は前年比約15％まで上昇。原油や石化製品などの高騰が、航空運賃など
のサービス価格や、輸送コストの上昇を通じた幅広い財価格に波及
◼ 旺盛なAI関連需要が物価を押し上げ
― データセンター投資需要を受けメモリー価格が高騰。PC・スマホ等の最終製品価格へも波及
― 需要拡大に伴う電力価格の上昇も相まって、供給ショックと投資需要が同時に物価を押し上げ

（注） 政府機関閉鎖による米CPIデータ欠損のため、2025年10月は欠値
（出所） OECDより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

高金利の常態化：政策金利に加え、タームプレミアムも金利を押し上げ
◼ 主要国・地域の中銀はインフレ警戒から利上げモードへ移行
― ホルムズ危機長期化や投資需要の強さに伴うインフレ圧力の高まりを受け、日米欧の中銀はいずれも9月に利上げ
◼ 日本以外の主要国においても、政策金利の上昇織り込みや財政懸念を背景に長期金利は上昇
― 米国では財政懸念に加え、足元では政策金利の上昇織り込みが長期金利を押し上げ

### Page 13

成長・負担の偏在が広げる「耐性格差」―4つの緊張関係
（出所） みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
②企業・家計間の緊張
生産性上昇
企業収益増加
遅れる賃金
・所得への波及
③企業間の緊張
大企業
／AI・戦略分野
中小企業
／非AI・戦略分野
④家計間の緊張
資産保有層
／高所得層
資産非保有層
／中低所得層
①国家間の緊張
技術／資本／資源
を持つ国
技術／資本／資源
を持たない国
→
成長の実感乏しく、社会的受容性が低下
→
保護主義・産業政策競争が加速
→
消費・資産形成力の差が累積
→
投資余力の差が次の成長機会を左右
格差は一時的か、固定化・自己増幅するか
（耐性格差が強まる中での日本の立ち位置は？）
投資主導経済＋インフレ＋高金利 拡大する耐性格差成長・負担の偏在
偏在化する成長の果実「新常態」下の金融市場中東危機長期化、加速するAI・レジリエンス投資

### Page 14

「耐性格差」は固定化するのか―成長の持続性を見極める3つの評価軸
（出所） みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
見極める軸 ポイント 代表指標 含意
波及 成長の果実は広がるか
◼ 生産性
◼ 実質賃金
◼ AI導入率
◼ 中小企業収益
波及すれば成長の裾野が拡大
しなければ成長の偏りが固定化
機会 「持たざる側」も次の成長機会に参加できるか
◼ 労働移動
◼ 新規参入
◼ 資金調達格差
◼ 人的資本投資
参加できれば格差は流動的
できなければ勝者・敗者が固定化
底上げ 格差が残る中でも、弱い主体の生活水準は改善するか
◼ ジニ係数（再分配）
◼ リスキリング
◼ 資産形成
◼ 社会移動
改善すれば社会的受容性を維持
しなければ分断圧力が強まる
問われるのは格差そのものではなく、偏在が成長の持続性を損なうか
⇒成長の果実が広がり、次の機会と生活水準の改善につながるかが焦点
評
価
軸
と
そ
の
含
意
問
題
設
定
「波及・機会・底上げ」を通じて、成長の持続性をモニタリング
偏在化する成長の果実「新常態」下の金融市場中東危機長期化、加速するAI・レジリエンス投資

### Page 15

参考①（国家間）：競争力の基盤は国ごとに偏在、資源高は日本に逆風
◼ 国家の競争力は、資源・製造能力・技術力・資金動員力・市場規模など複数の基盤に左右
― 日本は製造能力・技術力に強みを持つ一方、資源面の脆弱性から資源高局面では交易条件が悪化。また、潤沢な資金
（金融資産）を有しているものの、国内成長分野への資金動員力に課題
― 台湾・韓国では資源高で輸入価格が上昇する一方、半導体などAI関連財の価格上昇が輸出価格を押し上げ、交易条件
を下支え
国家の競争力を支える5つの基盤
競争力を支える基盤 強みを有する国・地域
①資源
エネルギー・重要鉱物などを安定的に確保できる力
米国、中東、
中国、豪州
②製造能力
戦略財を大量・安定的に生産できる力

新技術・サービスを生み出す力
米国、中国、

中国
⑤市場規模
大きな内需を背景に新技術を普及・量産できる力

参考②（企業・家計間）：生産性向上に遅れる賃金、低下する労働分配率
◼ 日本では時間当たり生産性が上昇する一方、1人当たり実質賃金の伸びは限定的
― 働き方改革など労働時間の短縮も両者の乖離に影響。交易条件悪化による所得流出も実質賃金を下押しする一因に
― 対して、AIブームの恩恵が大きい米国や韓国では、労働生産性と実質賃金がともに上昇する好循環

◼ 日本の労働分配率は主要国・地域と比較しても低下傾向。企業収益の賃金への還元が今後の焦点
― 米国や韓国においても足元で低下がみられるが、AI需要の拡大による企業収益の伸びが主因
― 日本では雇用者報酬の伸びが緩やかであり、生産性向上の成果を賃金に繋げられるかどうかが課題
（注） 労働生産性は総労働時間あたりの実質GDP、実質賃金は雇用者1人あたり
（出所） OECDより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
労働生産性と実質賃金 主要国・地域における労働分配率の推移
（注） 自営業者の労働所得を調整した雇用者報酬の名目GDP比。韓国の2024年以降は
OECDのデータを用いて延長
（出所） 欧州委員会、OECDより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 17

参考③（企業間）：金利上昇への耐性は企業規模・業種で大きな格差
◼ 日本企業の投下資本利益率（ROIC）は規模・業種間で大きな格差
― 総じてみれば、企業規模が小さいほどROICが低い傾向（中堅企業は例外）。特に小規模企業は1.5％と低水準
― 業種別では、製造業の輸送用機械や、非製造業の不動産、電力・ガス、運輸・郵便、医療・福祉などでROICが低い傾向。
なお、情報通信や宿泊・飲食は大企業のROICが相対的に高い一方、小規模企業が低く、業種内の格差が大きい
◼ インタレストカバレッジレシオ（ICR）も規模・業種間で格差。ROIC・ICRともに低い規模・業種ほど金利上昇に脆弱な構造
― ICRも規模が小さいほど低位（中堅企業は例外）。業種別では、不動産、電力・ガス、運輸・郵便、医療・福祉等が低水準
（注） 投下資本利益率（ROIC）＝税引き後営業利益÷（有利子負債＋自己資本）。大企業
は資本金10億円以上、中堅企業は1億円以上10億円未満、中小企業は1千万円以上
1億円未満、小規模企業は1千万円未満

（出所） 財務省「法人企業統計調査」より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

（注） インタレストカバレッジレシオ（ICR）＝営業利益÷支払利息。大企業は資本金10億円
以上、中堅企業は1億円以上10億円未満、中小企業は1千万円以上1億円未満、小

低所得世帯 高所得世帯
参考④（家計間）：金融資産を保有する層と持たざる層の間でインフレ耐性に格差
◼ 所得階級によって資産形成余力に格差。低所得世帯ほど資産形成が進みにくく、インフレ耐性が脆弱
― 高所得世帯では貯蓄余力が大きい一方、低所得世帯では物価高による生活費の増加が重石となり、貯蓄率は低水準
― 低所得世帯では金融資産の蓄積が進みにくく、インフレによる実質所得の目減りを資産所得で補う余地も限定的
◼ 資産形成余力に加え、資産選択も二極化。インフレ下では資産格差が拡大し、インフレ耐性の差も広がりやすい構造
― 余裕資金のある高所得世帯は収益性重視のリスク資産投資に積極的。一方、中低所得世帯はリスク資産投資に消極的
― インフレは資産形成余力と資産選択の違いを増幅させ、資産格差とインフレ耐性の格差がさらに拡大する可能性
（注） 勤労者世帯の平均貯蓄率。平均貯蓄率＝貯蓄純増 ÷ 可処分所得 × 100。対象

世帯・項目の違いにより、GDPベースの貯蓄率とは一致しない
（出所） 総務省「家計調査」より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
所得別にみた貯蓄率（2021～25年平均） 年収別にみた金融資産の選択基準（2021～25年）
（注） 対象は金融資産を保有する二人以上世帯
（出所） J-FLEC「家計の金融行動に関する世論調査」より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

（１）米国：AIによる景気押し上げは当面継続。物価高から FRBは一時的に政策金利を引き上げへ
◼ 2026・27年の実質GDP成長率はいずれも前年比＋2.2％と予想
― 設備投資は、2027年入り後に巡航速度並みの伸び率へ回帰。ハイパースケーラーは投資効率の低下を意識し、AIの普
及ペースを見極めながら投資を継続
― 個人消費は減税政策や資産効果により底堅く推移。2027年入り後はインフレ減速に伴う実質所得の改善も支えに
◼ FRBは関税引き上げや原油高によるインフレ上振れリスクを警戒し、2026年内に追加で1回の利上げ。関税・原油高の影
響が一巡した後、 物価の十分な減速が確認される2027年後半以降に2回の利下げを予想

◼ 足元の景気は堅調。引き続き、AIを中心とする設備投資が景気のけん引役に
― 4～6月期のGDP成長率は前期比年率＋2.2％と巡航速度を上回る結果。設備投資に加え、 株高に伴う資産効果や

米国：低採用・低離職で人材獲得競争は限定的。足元の物価上昇には賃金以外の要因が影響

（２）欧州：2026年後半は財政拡張が下支え。2027年はインフレ減速で潜在成長率を上回る成長
◼ 2026年後半は、熱波を受けた生産抑制やインフレによる消費下押しなど逆風はありつつも、財政拡張が下支え

### Page 32

欧州：熱波による河川水位低下が景気下押し要因に
◼ 河川の水位低下による生産下振れが7～9月期の成長率を下押し
― 高温と少雨を背景に欧州各地では河川の水位が低下。内陸物流において重要な役割を担うライン川の一部地点では、6
月以降、船舶の積載量に制約が生じる水位が継続
― ライン川の水位がおおよそ100㎝を切ったあたりから10㎝低下するごとにドイツの生産が0.4％程度減少すると試算
― 実際、船舶の輸送機能低下による原材料の供給制約を受けて化学・鉄鋼等の一部素材業種では生産抑制の動き。7～
9月期の経済下押し要因に
ライン川の水位とドイツ鉱工業生産の関係
（注） 閾値モデルを用いてライン川の水位がドイツ鉱工業生産に与える影響を推計。縦軸
の鉱工業生産は景気要因をコントロール（ドイツ鉱工業生産を世界の鉱工業生産に

欧州：エネルギー・食料品価格の上昇が、さらなるインフレ期待の上昇を招くリスク
◼ 天然ガスや農作物価格上昇の影響は今年後半から来年のインフレ押し上げ要因に
― 熱波による発電所の停止や農作物の不作懸念等を背景に、欧州の天然ガス・農産物の先物価格は上昇。これらの価格
上昇がインフレに与える影響は、先行き1年程度残存する計算

― 需給が緩和するにつれ、エネルギー・食料品そのものによる物価押し上げ幅は縮小へ。ただし、インフレ期待の上昇を招

### Page 36

（３）新興国：中国は景気減速が継続。アジアは原油調達の安定や AIブームが追い風に
◼ 中国は景気の減速傾向が続く見通し。2026年の成長率は4.3％、2027年は3.8％まで低下すると予想
― 内需は力強さを欠く。消費は補助金の反動で低迷が継続。投資も過当競争や地方財政難を受けて振るわず
― 外需は景気を下支え。EVなどの製品で輸出攻勢が続き、純輸出がプラスに寄与
◼ アジアでは原油の調達不安が後退、AIブームが景気をけん引。一方、インフレ・金利上昇圧力の高まりには留意が必要
― 原油調達は、代替輸入やバイオ燃料等の活用で懸念が後退。AIブームによる財輸出や域内投資のけん引が継続
― 他方、原油高の再燃やAIブームの継続がインフレ圧力を高める構図。金融政策は引き締め方向へ緩やかにシフト

中国：政府目標は達成圏内だが成長の勢いは弱い。内需が力強さを欠くなか、デフレ圧力は継続
◼ 2026年上期の実質GDP成長率は前年比＋4.7％。政府目標（＋4.5％～5.0％）の範囲内だが、内需の弱さが目立つ結果
― 4～6月期は同＋4.3％と2022年Q4（＋3.0％）以来の低水準。中東情勢を受けた供給ショックもあり、最終消費と総資本
形成が前期から減速。内需の弱さを純輸出（外需）が補う構図は変わらず

中国：原油輸入量は大幅減も、依然供給不足には至らず。代替燃料を用いた生産も拡大
◼ ホルムズ海峡の封鎖により、原油の輸入量は2ケタ減が続く。ただ、需要の低迷もあり供給不足には陥らず
― 海上輸送による原油輸入（3～9月）は封鎖前（10月～2月）対比▲45.0％。ロシアからの代替輸入も限定的
― ガソリンの消費量は3～8月に前年比▲8.8％、ディーゼルは同▲10.7％と低迷しており、需給のひっ迫を回避

― 加えて、国内生産量が豊富な石炭化学によるエチレン生産も強化しており、ナフサクラッカーの制約を緩和

中国：消費・投資の低迷が続く一方、輸出と生産は堅調。外需に成長を依存する構図が継続
◼ 消費・投資は減速に歯止めかからず。内需は一段と低迷
― 投資は前年比▲10％超の減少が継続。不動産不況、過当競争、地方財政難によるインフラ投資減速が背景
― 小売も減速傾向が継続。補助金の反動による自動車販売の減少等が下押し
― 一方、生産は引き続き底堅く推移。堅調な輸出を追い風に、コンピューター・通信機器などを中心に高い伸び継続
◼ 輸出はAIブームと自動車の輸出攻勢が継続。低迷する内需を外需の伸びで支える構図
― 輸出拡大は産業政策による高度化の成果と言える一方、貿易不均衡の拡大に対する懸念も（後述）

中国：好調な輸出の一方で輸入は停滞。背景に「自立自強」の進展
◼ 輸入は2010年代中盤以降に頭打ち。「自立自強」による輸入代替の進展が背景にあると考えられる
― かつて加工貿易が主流であった頃は、輸出製品を生産するため中間財や資本財の輸入が増え、先進国にも恩恵
― 世界の輸出入に占める中国のシェアは、輸出がほぼ一貫して上昇している一方、輸入は2010年代中盤以降に頭打ち
― 世界の輸入に占める中国のシェアを品目別に見ても、2024年は多くの業種で2015年からシェアが縮小
― 輸出伸長、輸入停滞の結果、貿易不均衡が拡大。先進各国はこれに懸念表明。貿易摩擦が再び強まるおそれも

アジア：原油高とAIブーム影響で生産コストが膨張。消費者価格への転嫁でインフレ圧力が高まる
◼ 企業の生産コストは増大。原油高を受けてエネルギーや石油由来製品の価格が急騰するほか、AIブーム下のメモリ高騰を
背景に、コンピューターや電気機器類の価格にも上昇圧力
― 主要国の生産者物価は総じて上昇ペースが加速。石油や有機化学製品、コンピューター類の品目が押し上げ
◼ 生産コストは消費者価格に波及。原油高がエネルギー費目を押し上げるほか、スマホやPCの価格も一部でインフレ要因に

― 幅広い地域でエネルギー部門がインフレ率を押し上げ。ガソリンや燃料（LPガス等）のほか、航空運賃の上昇が寄与
― 寄与度は小さいものの、スマホやPCなどの電子機器価格も上昇。メモリ価格の高騰が最終品価格に波及している模様
消費者物価：原油高・AIブーム影響

◼ インフレ圧力と通貨安のもと、金融政策は引き締めにシフト。ただし、内需下押し懸念から政策スタンスは域内でまちまちに

（４）日本：26年度は中東情勢影響のなかでも堅調に推移。 27年度は政策が消費・投資を押し上げ
◼ 2026年度の実質GDP成長率は前年比＋0.8％と予測。中東情勢緊迫化の影響を受ける一方、AI関連需要が下支え
― 中東情勢緊迫化は、原油価格上昇による交易利得・損失の悪化（実質的な所得の海外流出）や先行き不透明感の高ま
りを通じ、消費・投資を抑制。一方、AI・半導体関連輸出の拡大や投資促進税制の開始を受け、堅調な成長に
◼ 2027年度は同＋1.3％と予測。消費税減税の開始による個人消費の押し上げに加え、国内投資拡大がけん引

― 消費税減税が物価上昇の抑制を通じて実質賃金の改善要因に。また、政府の「強く豊かな日本投資枠」による戦略17分
野への支援開始に伴い、設備投資の拡大が成長率を押し上げると予想
（注） 網掛けは予測値

### Page 49

日本：コアCPIは2026年度末にかけて一時前年比＋3％程度に。日銀は2％まで利上げ継続
◼ AIブームの恩恵を受け、企業業績は中東情勢影響のなかでも改善傾向を維持
― 企業の好業績や人手不足の強さを背景に、2027年度の春闘でも5％程度の高い賃上げ率が続くと予想
◼ コアCPI（生鮮食品を除く総合）は、2026年度末にかけて一時的に前年比＋3％程度へ加速
― 原油価格の上昇が消費者物価を押し上げ。物価上振れリスクに対応するため、日銀は2％まで追加利上げを実施へ
― 2027年度に入ると、原油価格が徐々に落ち着くほか、利上げがインフレ率の上昇を抑制。加えて消費税減税が物価を一
段と下押しし、コアCPIが一時的に前年比＋1％台半ばへ低下する見込み
（注） 1. 網掛けは予測値。変化率は、水準のデータからみずほ総合研究所調査部が計算した値であり、公表値と一致しないことがある
2. 経常利益は法人企業統計調査の全規模・全産業（除く金融・保険）ベース
3. 消費者物価の「除く制度要因」は、政府による電気・ガス料金支援、ガソリン補助・暫定税率廃止、高校授業料無償化、消費税減税等の影響を除いて計算

日本：2026年度の成長率を上方修正。AI関連を中心とする輸出の堅調さが主な要因
◼ 2026年度の実質GDP成長率（＋0.8％）は、前回6月見通し（＋0.5％）対比で0.3％Ptの上方修正
― 米国を中心とする旺盛なAI関連需要の恩恵を受け、財輸出が堅調に拡大するとの見方が、26年度上方修正の主因
― 輸入価格上昇を受けた交易利得・損失の悪化（実質的な所得の海外流出）は、個人消費や設備投資などの抑制要因と
して残るものの、輸出の堅調な拡大が景気下押し圧力を一部緩和
◼ 2027年度は、消費税減税の開始や投資促進政策の効果発現により、引き続き高い成長を予想
― ただし、インフレ率の上振れを受けて実質所得の伸びがやや弱まることなどから、前回6月対比で小幅に下方修正

日本：政府が掲げる「2040年度：250兆円」の国内投資目標は、概ね実現可能な範囲
◼ 投資促進税制（26～28年度）に加え、戦略17分野を中心として「強く豊かな日本投資枠」（27～40年度）による支援を実施
― 「強く豊かな日本投資枠」は、27年度概算要求ベース（一般・特別会計）で14.2兆円と大規模
◼ 設備投資は自然体でも2040年度に230兆円へ到達する計算。政府目標は概ね現実的な範囲
― 直近実績を踏まえ「自然体」の伸びを＋4％とすると、目標達成に必要な押し上げ分は累計125兆円（年平均8兆円強）。

日本：輸入増の影響を考慮しても、投資拡大が名目GDPの水準を＋0.5％程度押し上げ
◼ 日本の資本財供給では輸入浸透度が上昇。グローバルな資本財需給ひっ迫は国内供給力強化の足かせに
― とりわけ、電気機械・情報通信機械では輸入依存度の高まりが顕著
― 半導体等を中心にグローバルな需給ひっ迫が続くとみられ、AI・半導体分野等を中心に投資完了には時間
◼ もっとも、輸入増による需要の海外漏出を考慮しても、国内生産増を通じたGDP押し上げ効果は相応の規模
― 投資需要が期待通り実現すれば、投資促進策がない場合と比べて名目GDP水準を平均＋0.5％程度押し上げ

日本：原油の代替調達は継続。ただし、油価再上昇や輸送コスト増加が物価上振れ要因に
◼ 今後も原油の代替調達が継続する見通し。時期により調達先が変化するものの、原油の供給不足は回避

日本：コスト高を受け、コアCPIは26年度末にかけて加速。27年度は消費税減税が下押し要因に

― 原油高による前年比押し上げの縮小や、日銀の利上げ効果、円安一服による輸入コスト上昇圧力の緩和が影響

日本：26年度後半の個人消費は緩慢な回復に留まるも、 27年度は消費税減税を追い風に拡大
◼ 2026年度後半はインフレ加速により、実質賃金の伸びが鈍化。個人消費は緩慢な回復に留まる見通し
― 個人消費は、良好な所得環境にもかかわらず低調。背景には、中東情勢の悪化に伴う消費者マインドの弱含み
― マインドは足元で回復の兆しも、26年度後半はインフレ加速が消費を抑制。消費税減税前の買い控えも下押し
◼ 2027年度の個人消費は消費税減税を追い風に拡大。買い控え後の反動増もあり、1％を上回る高い伸びとなる見通し

― 29年度の減税終了時には消費税率の再引き上げが物価を押し上げるも、新たな給付策で実質所得の落ち込みを緩和

金融政策：インフレ圧力の下で価格転嫁行動が積極化。日銀は 27年央に2％まで利上げ
◼ コスト高・供給不安定化のもと、日本企業の価格転嫁行動に積極化の兆候
― 民間調査では幅広い業種における価格転嫁短期化が示されているほか、足元では価格転嫁の動きに広がり。値上げ行
動の積極化は、収益改善を支えるとともに、物価上昇圧力の定着に繋がる可能性
◼ 物価上振れ警戒モードの金融政策運営が継続。9月会合後も日銀は短いインターバルで利上げ
― 9月会合後の会見で植田総裁は、物価引き上げから物価上振れ抑制へと「政策の局面が変化した」と説明

― 27年央にかけ四半期に1回程度のペースで利上げ。堅調な資金需要・投資計画を踏まえ、ターミナルレートは2％と予想
（注） 品目別に前年比の伸びを計算し、3カ月前から加速した品目の割合から減速した品
目の割合を差し引いて算出した指数

長期金利：市場の政策金利織り込みは2％に収束へ。長期金利は3％超の水準で高止まり
◼ 2026年度の長期金利は、物価上振れ懸念と利上げ織り込みの進展を背景に上昇
― タームプレミアム（上乗せ金利）は物価上振れ懸念から夏場にかけて大きく拡大したが、その後は緩やかな拡大ペースに
◼ 2027年度以降の長期金利は横ばい圏で推移。3％超の水準が常態化する見通し
― 物価上振れ懸念が後退するもとで、行き過ぎた利上げ織り込みは徐々に縮小
― 一方、日銀のバランスシート縮小を背景にイールドカーブの正常化が進展。日銀の大規模な国債保有で抑制されていた
タームプレミアムが緩やかに再拡大し、利上げ織り込みのはく落による金利低下圧力をおおむね相殺

（注） 政策金利織り込みは、今後10年間の政策金利に対する市場織り込みの平均。網掛け
部は期末政策金利との差分。2026年Q2までNakajima (2025)による推計値、2026年
Q3期以降みずほ総研推計値
（出所） Nakajima, J. (2025). “Impact of US monetary policy spillovers and yield curve
control policy,”、一上響・上野陽一 (2013). 「ゼロ金利下におけるタームプレミアム
の推計：日米英の長期金利の分析」、LSEGより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

為替：ドル円レートのレンジは2022年頃に円安方向へシフト。今後も構造的な円安圧力が残存
◼ 長い目でみれば、ドル円レートは2022年ごろを境にレンジが円安方向にシフト
― 2000～21年のドル円のレンジは1ドル＝80円～120円程度だったが、2022年以降は1ドル＝130円～170円程度に
― 背景には、①高い米金利、②円の需給環境の悪化、③日本のインフレノルムの定着など
◼ 先行きも、1ドル＝150円程度を中心とした円安水準が継続しやすい経済環境に
― 27年度は、短期的には「米利下げ」＋「日銀利上げ」で日米金利差が縮小し、円高要因に
― ただし、原油高が続くもとでの日本の貿易赤字、期待インフレの高止まり等が円安要因となり、大幅円高は見込まず
（出所） LSEGより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

• 日米金利差が縮小し、短期的には

• もっとも長期的には、日米成長率格
差から米金利が相対的に高水準
短期：円高
長期：円安
需給
• 短期的にはAI関連輸出が下支えす

• 原油高長期化で、輸入は高止まり
• デジタル赤字は拡大へ
• 対外直接投資も増加基調継続へ

期待

• 2010年代までは0%台だったが、

• 期待インフレ率の高止まりは、購買
力平価の観点では円安要因

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 美國 9 月 ISM 非製造業 PMI 從 8 月的 55.4 降至 54.9，顯示服務業活動增速放
緩，然而國內需求強勁使供應鏈承壓，並推動投入價格指數由 72.6 升至 74.0 為
四年多來新高；另一方面，美國 10 月密西根大學消費者信心指數由 9 月的 48.1
降至 46.3，為連續第三個月下降且創 5 月以來最低 ， 惟未來一年通膨預期由 4.6%
升至 4.7%，顯示通膨可能持續維持高位至 2027 年。
⚫ 聯準會 9 月 FOMC 會議紀要顯示與會官員均支持升息，並認為年底前尚需升息
一次 ，但對於後續升息時點及升息理由上存在分歧； 其中部分官員認為升息是防
止能源及其他價格衝擊造成更廣泛影響 的預防性措施，但更為鷹派官員則認為
升息在於抑制投資和支出，以防範正在顯現的需求驅動型通膨。
⚫ 歐洲央行 9 月貨幣政策會議紀要顯示全體決策委員均同意升息，並認為當前經
濟成長風險偏向下行 ， 通膨風險則偏向上行 ，因此將保留政策彈性， 根據經濟數

據做為決策，不提供未來利率路徑指引。
⚫ 台灣 9 月 CPI 年增率自 8 月 2.05%上升至 2.73%，已連續 5 個月超過 2%通膨警
戒線 ， 且創兩年半來最大漲幅 ，除了豪雨導致蔬果價格上漲及中秋假期的短期因
素外，油價居高不下亦使通膨壓力增加。
⚫ 中國央行發布匯率政策立場聲明， 強調中國貿易發展根源於產業國際競爭力的
提升，無必要通過匯率貶值獲取貿易競爭優勢 ，並指出中國長期實施以市場供需
為基礎、參考一籃子貨幣進行調節管理浮動匯率制度。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Oct/12/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.699，低點在 31.940，以 31.875 作收，較前一週收盤價
貶值 4.5 分。 
上週台幣震盪走貶，週初，受到前週公布的美國非農就業數據低於預期，降低
市場對聯準會 10 月升息的預期， 台股創高吸引外資匯入 ， 推升台幣最高來到31.699；
不過隨著美伊緊張情勢升溫且台灣連假將至 ， 台股收低使外資轉為大幅賣超並匯出 ，
推低台幣回到 9 月中以來的震盪區間下緣，最低觸及 31.940，最終在央行調節之下
收在 31.875。 
＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國 10 月密西根大學消費者信心
指數持續下滑，由 9 月的 48.1 降至 46.3，創 5 月以來最低，惟未來一年通膨預期
升至 4.7%，加上中東局勢反覆，油價高位震盪，仍支撐市場對於聯準會年內升息
的預期，預計美元指數維持在 102 附近震盪，對台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，
台灣 9 月出口創歷史新高，年增 60.9%，反映 AI 及科技需求持續強勁，出口商後
續拋匯需求可望提供台幣支撐；然而，台股逼近五萬點關卡之際，外資未能延續買
超走勢，且連假前已大幅賣超台股，若後續外資持續匯出，將限制台幣升值空間。
綜合以上，預估本週台幣區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.700-32.000

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Oct/12/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣區間震盪 。 中國十一長假期間 (10/1~10/7) 境內人民幣市場休市 ， 而
境外人民幣維持偏強格局 ， 主要在6.7000 上方震盪 ， 臨近周末 ， 人行中間價為6.7330，
創 3 年多來新高，加上美元指數漲勢放緩，推升境外人民幣突破 6.7000 整數關卡，
終場境外人民幣收在 6.6927，週線升值 134 點，境內人民幣收在 6.6929，較十一長
假前夕升值 121 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週人民幣區間偏升。就國際市場來看，美國密西根大學消費者信心指數
由 9 月的 48.1 降至 46.3，創5 月以來新低 ， 不過對於未來一年的通膨預期升至4.7%，
加上油價維持高檔 ， 市場對於聯準會年內升息的預期仍存 ， 美元指數維持高檔震盪 ，
將限制人民幣升值速度；就中國國內市場來看，人行中間價維持偏強設定，不過人
行表示將防範短期大幅波動與急貶風險 ， 並無意透過人民幣貶值取得貿易競爭優勢 ，
有助於穩定市場對人民幣的預期 ；最後就技術面來看 ，USD/CNH 日線圖中 ， 各天期
均線仍向下發散，空頭格局不變，下方支撐先看向 9 月底低點 6.6908。綜合以上，
預估本週人民幣區間偏升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6800-6.7100

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Oct/12/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY 震盪走升。週初，因美元指數在102 附近盤整，帶領USD/JPY 延
續前一週走勢震盪走升到 158 上方；週間，因缺乏重要經濟數據，市場偏向觀望，
USD/JPY 維持在 158 附近震盪；臨近週末，USD/JPY 曾一度回測 157.50 附近，不
過在美日利差的主導下，USD/JPY 依舊保持偏多的走勢，終場收在 158.31，週線上
漲 84 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、路透短觀調查顯示，受半導
體強勁訂單推動，日本 10 月大型製造業信心升至正 22，創近五年新高；但原料成
本高漲重擊非製造業，信心指數降至正 23。與此同時，此前投下升息反對票的日銀
鴿派委員佐藤綾野立場軟化，表態支持分階段升息，市場對後續正常化預期升溫，
預計將限縮 USD/JPY 的上行空間 。二 、 日本首相高市早苗表示密切關注匯率與物價
走勢 ， 強調日本已脫離通縮 ， 將完全尊重央行政策獨立性 ， 暗示不反對進一步升息 。
面對通膨加劇與日圓走貶壓力，其過往堅持的貨幣寬鬆立場已出現轉向。然而目前
美日實質利差仍大，持續為 USD/JPY 提供支撐。三、就技術面而言，日線圖中，
USD/JPY 持續沿著上彎的月線上攻，然匯價持續承壓於季線下方，在有效站上季線
及突破前高壓力前，仍以區間震盪看待。綜合以上，預估USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震
盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：157.10-159.50。

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Oct/12/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 震盪 走跌 。週初， 因法國債券風險與區域政治不確定性 ，
EUR/USD 下行最低來到 1.1160； 週間 ，受中東地緣風險升溫與歐洲財政疑慮帶動避
險需求 ， 加上美債殖利率維持高檔 ， 美元利差優勢延續，歐元維持低檔； 臨近週末 ，
逢低歐元有買盤支撐，帶動歐元收復部分跌幅，終場收在 1.1201，週線下跌 46 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 區間偏空震盪，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行首席經濟學
家連恩表示，夏末能源成本飆升為經濟增長帶來下行風險，並可能限制央行收緊貨
幣政策的力道，與此同時，歐元區 10 月 Sentix 投資者信心指數由 5.1 降至 2.7，德
國預期指數同步滑落，反映市場對經濟復甦的信心明顯降溫，預計將令歐元持續承
壓。二、法國社會動盪與支出訴求阻礙財政減赤進程，加劇債務可持續性疑慮，並
推升法德公債利差與信用風險，令歐元承壓走弱。相較美國經濟展現韌性且聯準會
維持偏鷹立場，歐洲深陷景氣放緩與財政掣肘，歐洲央行緊縮空間受限。在歐美基
本面與政策前景持續分歧下 ，EUR/USD 缺乏實質支撐 ， 後市將維持偏弱整理並下探
關鍵支撐。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 匯價雖多次向下測試 1.1165 關
鍵價位均獲得支撐，惟每次反彈均無法站上五日線，且各天期均線下彎呈現空頭排
列。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD 本週呈現區間偏空震盪的可能性較高。

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分 ， 本週邁入新的提存期 ， 央行連日減發存單 ， 令市場資金供
給充裕，帶動短率朝區間低位移動。

市場預期區間 1.61%-1.635%，較上期標售結果下跌 4.1 個基本點。 
週 四 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.830% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.67%~1.68%，利率大致持平於上週。 
二、 受美債利率續創新高影響，週四 10 年期指標券 A15109 成交在 2.05%，創同
年期券自 2008 年以來高點，5 年期指標券 A15108 則未成交，最近成交利率
為 1.82%。 
台灣 9 月 CPI 年增率自 8 月 2.05%上升至 2.73%，已連續 5 個月超過 2%通膨
警戒線 ， 且創兩年半來最大漲幅 ， 目前油價仍居高難下 ， 通膨壓力將令央行維
持偏緊的貨幣政策，加上國際債市利率持續走升，台債殖利率恐面臨上行壓
力，預估 5 年券區間為 1.85%~1.95%，10 年券區間為 2.0%~2.10%。

### Page 3

3
Cathay’s View市場策略思維
市場焦點
1. 市場焦點:
• 中東戰火升溫, WTI高位震盪: 伊朗加大船隻攻擊, 胡塞攻擊沙國, WTI一度站上百元大關。傳聞俄烏露
出談判曙光, 油價自高點回落, 但運費高漲, 研判油價仍將高位整理。
• 聯準會10月可望維持利率不變, 美債利率保持高位: 中東局勢升溫與AI融資疑慮升高, 資金流入美債避
險, 加上官員釋出觀望訊號, 美債殖利率自高點回落, 但通膨壓力仍在, 使美債利率高檔震盪。
• AI需求依舊強勁, 但企業獲利動能高峰已過: 超微電腦、SpaceX及Meta均釋出AI需求強勁的訊號, 緩解
AI泡沫疑慮, 不過, 考量獲利增速高峰已過, 可分散佈局, 降低市場波動壓力。
2. 後續看點:
• 3大CSP財報(10/27-10/28)、FOMC利率會議(10/29)、ECB政策會議(10/29)、台灣GDP(10/30)
策略思維
1. 股市−多頭不變, 但高利率增添評價波動: 殖利率高位, 評價面持續承壓, 使股價落後基本面表現。不過, 企業

營收獲利未來成長空間仍大, 股市仍有高點可期, 惟獲利成長高峰已過, 盡量分散各產業。
2. 債市−長端不確定性仍高, 宜以中短天期或公司債為佳: 通膨高於預期, 加上政府債務壓力, 長率仍有上行壓
力, 建議控制債券部位存續期間, 選擇短天期或浮動債較佳。
3. 匯市−美元走揚與股市震盪, 引發亞幣走弱: 美元雙率走高提高外資賣超亞股, 亞幣偏弱看待。

主要中東油國產油持續回升 航運運費居高不下
原油｜主要中東油國產油雖持續回升, 但航運運費居高不下、墊高原油成本, 讓油價難明顯回落
• 中東主要產油國的原油生產逐步修復 , 包括沙烏地阿拉伯 、阿聯等國生產持續回升 。這意味著全球原油供給端的
緊縮壓力已較上個季度改善。從供需平衡的角度來看, 產油回升理應為油價帶來下行空間。
• 然而, 運輸環節卻抵消上述供給改善的影響。中東航道的安全風險持續存在,部分油輪則改道好望角, 種種因素均推
升運費水準。高昂的航運成本成為原油到岸價格的重要墊高因素, 使得即使產地價格出現鬆動, 最終到達消費地的
成本仍居高不下。這種「產地供給改善、運抵成本居高」落差, 讓市場難以單純透過產量回升來消化價格壓力。
• 供給回升與運費高漲、地緣風險難解的交互作用下, 原油價格較難明顯回落。油價可能延續高位震盪, 並對全球通
膨預期與央行政策形成牽制。

資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/20。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/20。

中東戰爭以來, 中國原油進口下降 今年以來, 中國油品與塑料出口價格走升
原油｜中國原油進口下降, 雖有助舒緩原油供需緊俏, 但禁止油品出口, 卻讓需求國油品價格難降
• 中國作為全球最大原油進口國 , 中東戰爭以來進口量有所回落 (因先前低油價時期購入大量被制裁原油 , 積累高達
12~14億桶庫存, 因此戰後選擇不當高價油的接受者), 為原油供需緊張局面注入一絲緩解空間, 並在一定程度上抑
制原油現貨價格的上行動能。從宏觀供需平衡來看, 這本應為原油市場帶來溫和的修正空間。
• 然而, 中國10月實施油品出口限制, 在下游成品環節形成不同影響。受到戰爭影響, 中東部分煉油、裂解產能受到
影響, 加上美國產輕甜油但多數煉油與裂解產能適合以重油提煉, 使得對煉油價差、油品等報價難降。雪上加霜的

是, 中國10月初宣布, 因應中東局勢與國內庫存偏低, 暫停成品油外銷, 使整體成品油市場更形緊俏。
• 整體來說,「原油轉鬆、成品仍緊」的結構性背離持續, 對消費者能源價格的僵固性將更為明顯, 也對仰賴進口成品
油的經濟體影響更為艱難。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/20。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/20。
萬噸 Index (2020/1 =100) Index (2020/1 =100)

短線升息時點遞延, 後續緊縮方向未改 預估10月維持不變, 今年底、明年Q1各升息一碼
資料來源: Bloomberg, 國泰世華銀行整理, 截至2026/10/16。 資料來源: Bloomberg預估, 截至2026/10/20。
• 10月FOMC料按兵不動, 未來將進一步緊縮: 9月升息一碼後, 近期Fed官員普遍認為無須急於再次升息, Fed將持續
觀察貨幣政策對物價控制、金融狀況的影響; 惟後續升息路徑尚未結束。
• 能源衝擊外溢, 物價壓力支撐後續升息路徑: 美國9月CPI月增0.6%、PPI月增1.3%, 能源供應短缺的衝擊逐步擴散。
褐皮書亦顯示, 柴油、燃料成本正沿著供應鏈傳導, 使原物料、關稅、保險成本持續推升企業價格壓力。
• 短線升息預期降溫, 反映時點遞延: 預料12月升息一碼, 明年Q1亦保留再升一碼空間; 而明年3月將受高基期影響,
有助通膨數據(年增)呈現降溫。
FOMC前瞻｜預期10月Fed按兵不動, 年底升息可能性仍高

官員 評論
Kevin Warsh
(主席, 10/16)
不預判10、12月路徑, 透過任務小組以研議政策框架 。
重申物價穩定仍為核心, 同時關注AI投資需求帶來的
通膨、金融穩定風險。
Beth Hammack
(克里夫蘭, 10/16) 金融狀況大致保持中性 , 若通膨惡化將需更積極升息 。
Mary Daly
(舊金山, 10/15) 反覆價格衝擊將滲透至通膨預期 , 通膨風險偏向上行 。
Michelle Bowman
(副主席, 10/14)
通膨仍高於目標, 9月升息後無需急於行動, 可先觀察
金融環境的緊縮程度。
Thomas Barkin
(里奇蒙, 10/14) CPI仍高於理想水準, 需更多數據確認, 但不預設立場。
John Williams
(紐約, 10/13) 年內再度升息為合適, 但9月升息後無需急於行動。

殖利率創近年新高後, 長債買盤回溫、曲線趨平 財政赤字惡化、公債供給壓力延續
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/20。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/14。
• 10月升息預期降溫, 前期債市賣壓暫獲舒緩: 先前較激進的升息路徑逐漸修正, 以及資金對美債的避險偏好回升, 帶
動殖利率自高點回落。另方面, 隨殖利率升至相對高檔, 亦增添債券配置吸引力: 10/9拍賣10年期美國公債需求強
勁, 投標倍數2.77倍創近十年來高點, 並且直接投標人、間接投標人合計獲配比例97.4%創史上新高。
• 縱使近期買盤回流, 但整體「利率高原」格局未改: 1)中東衝突與原油供給風險, 使通膨及Fed升息路徑仍具不確定
性; 2)美國財政赤字、公債供給壓力延續, 加上AI投資推升企業發債需求, 結構性因素仍限制長端利率下行空間。

市場評估AI融資風險, 近期信用利差再度走擴 槓桿率較高AI業者利率走升
註: 圖表以2026/1=100標準化, 目前利差IG=86bps、HY=332bps、CCC=1085bps。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/20。
註: 槓桿率採Net Debt/EBITDA, CoreWeave約為9.5x, Oracle為3.8x, Hyperscalers僅介於
-0.7x~0.7x。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/20。
• 近期公債利率快速下滑, 信用債利率同步回落, 惟降幅相對有限導致信用利差走擴。另方面, 在融資成本升高下, 市
場持續檢視AI業者的ROI, 以及營收、現金流能否覆蓋龐大Capex及利息支出, 成為信用風險的定價關鍵。
• 壓力集中於高槓桿業者: 大型Hyperscalers具較強內部現金流, 近期壓力集中在高度依賴外部融資、槓桿率更高的
業者; CoreWeave信用疑慮升高(債務規模龐大、Q2淨虧損), Oracle FCF承壓、市場亦擔憂降評風險。

• AI投資的雙向信用風險: AI Capex維持高檔, 持續推升企業發債與融資需求; 但更大尾端風險在於, 若Hyperscalers
下修資本支出並減少外部算力需求, 雖有助改善自身FCF, 但將削弱Neocloud、資料中心等租賃算力業者的訂單。
• 債市布局: 高利率提升債券配置吸引力, 惟發行人分化加劇, 體質仍以IG為主、報酬著眼於穩定息收。

主要企業財報狀況
註: 超微電腦目前只公布財報初稿, 完成財報將於11/3公布。
資料來源: Bloomberg, , 國泰世華銀行整理, 截至2026Q3財報。
• 受到油價走揚、CoreWeave融資利率飆升等因素影響, 市場對AI泡沫的疑慮升溫, 科技股明顯回檔。不過, 隨著主
要科技企業陸續公布財報, 包括超微電腦、SpaceX 及 Meta 均釋出 AI 需求強勁的訊號, 帶動科技股逐步回升。
• 其中, SpaceX 本季營收達161億美元, 年增161%, 主要成長動能來自AI業務的爆發式成長, 營收從去年同期的8.1
億美元大幅攀升至75.6億美元, 甚至超越Starlink的連網業務。此外, 美國AI伺服器大廠超微電腦也受惠於強勁的
AI需求, 最新季度營收初步預估中位數達190億美元, 顯示 AI基礎建設需求仍具強勁動能。

獲利動能變動下的標普500表現 獲利動能變化下各類股表現
註: 標普500企業當季獲利年增率若高於上季, 表示加速, 反之為減速。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 從2000Q1-2026Q2。
註: 標普500企業當季獲利年增率若高於上季, 表示加速, 反之為減速。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 從2000Q1-2026Q2。
• 標普500企業財報陸續開跑, 銀行股已率先公布財報, 整體表現大致符合市場預期, 接下來市場焦點將轉向10月底
陸續公布財報的大型雲端服務供應商(CSP)。受惠於AI熱潮延續, 市場預期標普500企業Q3獲利仍將維持亮眼表現,
年增率可望達到30%。
• 值得留意的是, 儘管AI熱潮持續, 但隨著獲利基期墊高, 企業獲利成長動能的高峰可能已過, 後續增速恐難超越Q2
的50%。從過去經驗來看, 當企業獲利成長逐步放緩, 股市未來報酬率也可能隨之下降。此外, 在高利率環境下, 股
市波動風險仍不容忽視, 投資配置不妨適度提高防禦型類股比重, 包括核心消費與公用事業, 以提升投資組合的防

禦能力。

外銷訂單高速成長, AI需求趨勢不變 殖利率走升壓抑評價面

體需求動能仍強, 帶動台灣全年出口挑戰突破9000億美元。
• 殖利率上升不僅影響美股評價面, 也影響台股估值, 加上明年獲利成長減速的擔憂, 進一步拖累台股評價面, 台股預

帶動股市上漲。

記憶體｜回顧過去30年十次記憶體循環, 股價約領先獲利峰值一季見頂
資料來源: Bloomberg, 國泰世華銀行整理, 以上數據均為美光財報與股價表現, 截至2026/10/19。
• 根據歷史規律, 即便獲利成長率已見頂, 若獲利絕對金額仍能持續成長, 記憶體股價仍能夠持續創高。然而, 一旦獲

利金額見頂, 該波週期股價漲勢多半提前結束。過去週期股價平均約領先獲利3個月見頂, 換言之, 市場目前的定價
隱含記憶體廠的獲利將於2026Q3至Q4後開始季減。
成長率見頂, 漲勢仍有可能延續很久 獲利一旦見頂, 漲勢提前結束

DRAM廠商2027年產能擴增幅度有限 DRAM報價仍持續上漲
記憶體｜2027供給新增有限, 若AI需求並未放緩, 供不應求格局暫難扭轉
• 然而, 美光先前給出的樂觀展望與市場的預期出現明顯落差, 公司稱2027與2028的供需情形將較2026年更加嚴峻,
且FY27第一季度(2026/11)的毛利率將是FY27全年毛利率的地板 。另一方面, 目前記憶體合約價與現貨價仍持續
上漲, 需求並未出現明顯轉折。
• 即便2028年以後的AI需求是否放緩, 記憶體供不應求情形是否改變的確仍具備不確定性 , 但2027年各廠商新增產
能有限, 同時算力需求仍然強勁, 明年記憶體廠商獲利出現衰退的機率較低, 近期的劇烈回檔更偏向情緒驅動而非
基本面改變。
資料來源: J.P. Morgan預估, 截至2026/8/9。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/1。

• 日本9月出口年增16.7%, 連13個月維持正成長, 不過9月出口增速連兩月放緩, 也低於市場預期的20.1%, 搭配花旗
日本經濟驚奇指數自高檔回落, 顯示經濟成長動能略有放緩, 但仍呈現穩健成長。
• 近期高市首相在國會接受質詢時否認政策導致日圓疲弱和推高日本利率, 表示會適當地控制政府發債規模, 日本10
年債殖利率自3.1%高點回落至2.9%下方, 但仍是近30年來最高水準, 且央行總裁植田和男維持穩健升息論調, 預料
將於12月再度升息, 使日債殖利率維持相對高檔。

法國民眾關注焦點 法國信用持續惡化
資料來源: Bloomberg, IPSOS, 截至2026/9。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/21。
歐洲｜民怨烽火且預算卡關, 法債難平
• 法國政府試圖推動溫和減赤 , 卻在極左與極右陣營反對下面臨極高的倒閣危機 。信評機構穆迪於10月23日公布的
評級審查, 預料法國原有的「Aa3」評等面臨被直接調降至 「A1」的風險, 恐推升融資成本。此外10/27預算收入
案若再拖延, 法國10年期公債殖利率與法德利差恐持續惡化。
• 與此同時, 社會民怨也已達到臨界點, 今年酷暑熱浪重創核電供給, 導致民生管制電價在8月被迫調升2.5%, 進一步
剝奪了實質購買力 。民眾普遍認為惡化的公共服務與斷裂的福利合約 , 導致「深知債務迫切、卻拒絕承擔緊縮 」
的認知撕裂, 催生了10月中旬高達數十萬人的全國性暴力街頭抗爭。高昂的電費與生活成本積怨, 正與政治財政危

機相互綁定, 使法國陷入難以解套的惡性循環, 短期法債風險難以消除。

歐元區交易區間下移 歐美12月會議利率變動機率
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/21。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/21。
匯市｜歐元受事件衝擊後回神, 年底利率變動預期仍是關鍵
• 歐元兌美元跌破1.14並下移至新的交易區間後 , 目前呈現低檔震盪築底的態勢 。儘管法國國會僵局與穆迪即將到
來的降評風險等結構性問題依舊, 但市場在經歷先前的恐慌拋售後, 已對此利空進行了定價。畢竟歐元是集合體且
有德國托底, 隨著政治風暴對匯市的邊際影響逐漸減弱, 歐美兩大央行12月會議的「利差變動預期」將接棒成為驅
動歐元後續走勢的核心力道。
• 從近期市場預期來看 , 歐洲央行與聯準會雙方對貨幣政策路徑的角力正進入白熱化 。以目前通膨數據以及雙方官
員的表態, 12月利差難以擴大, 歐元可能繼續受制政治雜音於低位震盪, 但無序下跌的機率有限, 短期先看重回區間
上緣走勢。

市場焦點
美國
• 9月綜合PMI升至58.4，創2021年7月以來新高 ，製造業與服務業同步加速 ，新訂單及就業亦
明顯成長 ，顯示消費 、AI投資與國防支出支撐景氣動能 。不過，需求快速擴張也加劇供應鏈
瓶頸，企業投入價格升至 2022年10月以來高點，燃料、運輸及原物料成本上升 ，顯示景氣韌
性與通膨壓力同步升溫，增加Fed政策維持緊縮的壓力。
美國
期中選舉
• 目前民調顯示 ，民主黨可能取得兩院或眾議院多數 ，這將有助於縮減未來財政赤字預期 ，進
而帶動10年期美債殖利率下滑 ，對長天期美債形成支撐 。此外既有資料顯示 ，選後一年美股
平均報酬率達12.4%，主因為政治不確定性降低，市場焦點重新回到經濟成長、企業獲利及貨
幣政策等基本面因素，進而帶動投資信心提升。

越南
• 越南股市正式納入富時羅素新興市場指數 。估計此次納入最高可引導 60億美元的資金重新流
入越南。資產管理公司Vanguard也計畫在未來幾年內，將對越南的投資額提升至約 25億美元。
• 越南股市短期仍面臨逆風 ，除了能源通膨壓力居高不下恐拖累經濟成長 ，今年以來越南成為
美國最大貿易逆差國，加上美方企圖阻止越南成為中國商品流入的中轉國 ，美對越的301條款
調查為越南出口額帶來更多不確定性。

美國景氣強勢擴張，通膨與利率壓力再升
• 企業活動創逾五年新高 ，製造業與服務業同步加速擴張 ：S&P
Global美國9月綜合PMI初值升至58.4，創2021年7月以來新高，
且連續四個月加速成長；服務業 PMI升至58.7（59個月高點）、
製造業PMI升至57.0（52個月高點），產出亦同步加速 ，加上
訂單與就業成長皆達近年最快 ，顯示內需、AI投資及國防支出
共同支撐企業活動。S&P Global依PMI調查推估經濟年成長約
5%，Q3則上看4%，反映短期景氣動能明顯增強。
• 供給受限推高成本 ，通膨壓力再升溫： 需求快速擴張同時推高
供應鏈與產能壓力 ，企業未完成訂單升至 2022年5月以來高點 ，
供應商交貨延遲亦明顯惡化 ，反映產能不足 ，投入價格指數升

至2022年10月以來新高 ，企業主要反映燃料 、運輸、原物料
及薪資成本上升 ，顯示供給受限與能源成本可能持續向終端價
格傳導，增加通膨黏著風險並提高Fed維持緊縮政策的壓力。
• 後市觀察：數據公布後，10年期美債殖利率一度升至約 5.12%，
創2007年以來新高 ，市場對10月再升息的押注亦升溫 ，短線
需關注供應鏈壓力是否緩解 ，以及後續ISM、就業與物價數據
能否確認通膨再升溫趨勢 。投資策略建議關注利率敏感度較低
的高品質成長股或防禦型資產 ，可適度布局受惠強勁內需與 AI
／國防支出的美股大型科技與工業類股 ，並留意升息環境下金
融股的利差受益機會 ，整體建議維持分散配置並嚴控利率風險 。
資料來源：S&P Global PMI, Bureau of Economic Analysis via S&P Global Market
Intelligence， 2026.9.24

能源成本升高推升通膨，利率維持高檔壓力升溫
• 能源與運輸成本上升，增加通膨黏著風險：中東衝突與
俄羅斯煉油設施遭攻擊 ，使全球柴油供應持續偏緊 ，燃
料價格及運輸成本同步上升；由於柴油廣泛應用於運輸 、
農業與製造業，價格上漲將透過物流及生產成本向下游
傳導。目前全球柴油庫存偏低 、煉油產能受限，若供應
中斷延續，可能使能源價格維持高檔並延長通膨壓力。
• 公債與企業債供給增加 ，長端利率壓力升高：美國財政
赤字及政府融資需求偏高 ，加上大型科技公司持續為 AI
資料中心等基礎建設籌資 ，債券供給增加使市場承接壓
力升高；在通膨預期未完全穩定的情況下 ，投資人對長
天期債券要求較高的期限溢酬 ，進一步推升長端殖利率 ，
同時AI相關企業債近期亦出現利差擴大 ，反映市場對大

量發債及資本支出回收期的關注升高。
• 資產配置面臨成長與通膨雙重拉扯： 能源價格、企業融
資成本及長端殖利率若持續處於高檔 ，將提高企業資金
成本並壓縮高估值資產的評價空間；同時 ，利率與能源
價格波動也可能擴大股 、債市震盪。因此資產配置建議
降低長天期債券對利率變化的敏感度 ，提高對通膨與利
率波動的防禦能力 ，並以現金流穩定、具備定價能力之
優質標的為核心。
資料來源：Federal Reserve Bank of St. Louis via FRED ， 2026.9.8

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.9

美國期中選舉-目前民調顯示民主黨可能取得兩院或眾議院多數
• 目前民調顯示民主黨可能取得兩院或眾議院多數 ：最新民調顯示，民主黨
於11月美國期中選舉，取得國會多數席次的機率已領先共和黨，根據市場
情境分析，期中選舉不影響既有法案執行 ，但將左右未來兩年的財政預算 、
稅制改革 、政府支出及國會監督權限 。情境(1)若共和黨續掌國會 ，減稅
政策延長、產業回流、關稅及移民限制等議程有望加速推進 ，財政擴張力
道可能進一步增強 。情境(2)分裂國會 ，兩黨制衡將使重大法案推動難度
提高，新增減稅與大型財政刺激方案較難落地 。情境(3)民主黨全面掌控
國會，則可能提高對政府政策的監督力度 ，政策方向偏向提高稅負與監管 ，
壓抑企業獲利 ，使政策環境趨向平衡。

美國期中選舉-對於股債市的影響
• 對股市的影響：美國銀行分析1900至2025年共31次美國期中選舉，資料發現標普500在期中選舉前12個月平均報酬約為2.9%，
低於所有年度平均 8.9%。因為市場在選舉前往往受到政策不確定性 、政黨競爭及投資人觀望情緒影響 ，波動相對較大 。然而
此現象不代表選舉必然導致市場疲弱，因為當時的經濟環境、通膨、利率及企業獲利等因素同樣扮演重要角色。期中選舉後12
個月的股市表現通常較佳 。歷史數據顯示，選後一年美股平均報酬率達12.4%。主因為政治不確定性降低，市場焦點重新回到
經濟成長、企業獲利及貨幣政策等基本面因素，進而帶動投資信心提升。
• 對債市的影響：若民主黨取得國會主導權，市場普遍預期財政支出擴張幅度將較受節制 ，有助於縮減未來財政赤字預期，進而

帶動10年期美債殖利率下滑，對長天期美債形成支撐，若共和黨持續全面掌控國會，市場可能預期減稅及財政擴張政策延續，
進一步推升財政赤字預估，帶動公債殖利率走高，對長天期美債形成壓力，不過市場已部分反映相關預期，實際衝擊幅度有限。
美國期中選舉前後對於美股的影響
資料來源：美國銀行，2026.7
資料來源：復華投信，2026.9

AI建設熱潮吞噬自由現金流
• 資本支出創新高：雲端服務巨頭(包括亞馬遜、Alphabet、微軟、Meta、甲骨文以及SpaceX等)正在全力擴充AI算力與資料
中心，2026年大型雲端服務巨頭的資本支出合計預估將接近 9,600億美元，而2025至2031年的累計AI資本支出更可能逼近
9兆美元。
• 自由現金流出現赤字： 高額的投資加上股東回報(股息與庫藏股買回)，使許多巨頭的自由現金流(FCF)轉為負值(如Alphabet、
亞馬遜、微軟、Meta及甲骨文等)，預計到2027年，部分企業將更加依賴外部融資，而非單靠內部現金生成。
• 債券發行量大增： 為了填補資金缺口 ，科技業債券發行規模急遽上升 ，2026年迄今，各幣別的投資級科技債券發行量已突
破4,000億美元，創下歷史新高。

獲利韌性與金庫深度：強勁EBITDA成為信用安全網
• 上兆美元的流動性護城河： 儘管債務增加，但科技十二巨頭合計持有近 1兆美元的等值現金，整體科技板塊流動性充裕 ，短
期內無流動性危機。
• EBITDA強勁成長消化債務：市場預期覆蓋的12家最大科技企業在2026與2027年的EBITDA合計將成長約35%，強勁的獲利
能力顯著降低槓桿比率的上升壓力，確保信評機構維護穩定的信用評級。
• 資本配置策略分化：(1)兼顧股東回報：蘋果、微軟、Meta與Alphabet在投入AI同時，仍維持大規模的庫藏股買回與股息發
放。(2)全數投入資本支出：亞馬遜將絕大部分現金導向資本支出(2026年資本支出預計達2,200億美元居首)，且不發放股息。
• 小結與投資建議：科技巨頭為維持AI技術與基礎設施領先優勢，短期內，負自由現金流將成為常態。然而，這並非財務危機

訊號，而是企業利用超強獲利能力 (高EBITDA)與龐大的現金儲備進行戰略性擴張 ，科技板塊的信用風險目前處於可控且健
康的狀態，投資建議聚焦AI變現能力與EBITDA成長，優先佈局具有強大獲利護城河且能將算力轉化為實質營收的龍頭企業 。

越南股市正式納入富時羅素新興市場指數
• 預估引導60億美元資金流入越南： 富時羅素估計，此次納入最高可引導 60億美元的資金重新流入越南 。受此提振，資產管
理公司Vanguard也計畫在未來幾年內，將對越南的投資額提升至約25億美元。整個指數過渡將分為四個階段推行至2027年：
今年9月率先納入10%，明年3月追加20%，最後於明年6月與9月分別完成剩餘各35%的分配。市場分析指出，隨著明年3月
配額加大，市場關注度與對本地投資人的影響將更為顯著。
• 越南股市短期仍面臨逆風： 儘管官方早前誓言今年 GDP成長目標達10%，但中東衝突以來能源通膨壓力居高不下恐拖累經
濟成長。此外，越南與美國的貿易協商仍在進行中，由於今年以來越南成為美國最大貿易逆差國，加上美方企圖阻止越南成

為中國商品流入的中轉國，美對越的301條款調查為越南出口額帶來更多不確定性。
• 觀點：儘管短線面臨不確定性 ，但越南股市同時具備多項利多題材 ，包含企業獲利成長、公共投資加速、FDI回流及資本市
場改革等。中長期結構仍偏正向，可以衛星配置的角度布局，或是以定期定額的方式參與長期成長潛力。
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.09。

6. 本資料內容之著作權屬兆豐銀行所有，禁止就所載資料任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。
7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重 。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良
管理人之注意義務外不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。
11. 屬基金資訊觀測站ESG基金專區所列之基金，有關基金之ESG資訊，投資人應於申購前詳閱基金公開說明書或投資人須知所載之基金所有特色或目標等資訊。
警 語

## Document 062: 2026年9月经济数据点评：外强内弱延续，政策脉冲待传导

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年9月经济数据点评：外强内弱延续，政策脉冲待传导.pdf
Report source: 联合资信评估
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

www.lhratings.com  研究报告 1

外强内弱延续，政策脉冲待传导 —
2026 年 9 月经济数据点评 
联合资信 主权部  
前三季度 GDP 同比增长 4.6%， 三季度回升至4.5%， 经济在二季度减速后温和企稳。9 月
延续 “外需与高技术制造引领、 内需与传统部门偏弱” 的分化格局： 出口再创新高、AI 链主导
且动能向多品类扩散； 消费边际改善但汽车拖累显著， 投资与房地产仍在寻底； 工业增加值加
快，但增长高度集中于高技术制造；PPI 创年内新高，CPI 与 PPI 剪刀差重新扩大；新增社融
超预期但主要由政府债支撑， 信贷仍偏弱。 政策端增量工具加快落地， 但向实体需求的传导仍
待观察。整体处于政策驱动向内生修复过渡的“缺口期” ，修复持续性取决于消费与房地产的
企稳节奏。

### Page 2

www.lhratings.com  研究报告 2 
2026 年前三季度 GDP 同比增长 4.6%，分季度看，一季度增长 5.0%，二季度放
缓至 4.3%， 三季度回升至4.5%， 环比增长1.2%， 经济在经历二季度减速后于三季度
温和企稳。9 月数据延续“外需与高技术制造引领、内需与传统部门偏弱”的分化格
局： 出口再创新高、 工业增加值加快增长， 但消费仅边际改善， 固定资产投资与房地
产仍在寻底，PPI 与 CPI 剪刀差重新扩大，社融总量承压而结构优化。整体而言，经
济处于由政策驱动向内生修复过渡的“缺口期”，修复的持续性取决于消费与房地产
的企稳节奏，而非外需或政策脉冲的单一强度。 
一、出口：再创新高、AI 链主导、动能向多品类扩散 
9 月，按美元计价，中国出口总值同比增长 36.3%，前值为 25.0%；进口总值同
比增长 31.0%，前值为 28.2%；贸易顺差 1335.3 亿美元，较去年同期增加 448.1 亿美
元，创 2025 年 1 月以来最高水平。 
出口的强劲表现仍高度集中于 AI 相关产业链， 但增长动能开始向更多品类扩散。
9 月集成电路出口同比增长 136.8%， 增速较上月加快6.2 个百分点； 自动数据处理设
备及其零部件同比增长 93.3%，增速加快 16.3 个百分点，两者合计拉动出口增长约
13 个百分点。与此同时， 增长动能向更多品类扩散：传统消费品出口明显回暖， 玩具
增长 40.8%， 服装、 鞋靴、 家具分别增长24.1%、20.3%和 22.1%， 服装、 鞋靴、 箱包、
玩具和家具合计拉动出口增长 1.9 个百分点， 低基数效应及对美出口修复可能是重要
原因。 
分区域看，对东盟出口增速升至 52.3%， 对韩国出口增速达68.2%， 均创2021 年
2 月以来新高； 对美出口在低基数效应及中美经贸关系边际缓和支撑下回升至 39.6%，
增速创 2021 年 3 月以来新高；对欧盟出口增速大幅回升至 25.3%，为今年 2 月以来
最高水平， 但9 月中国对欧盟贸易顺差扩大至 348 亿美元， 创有记录以来新高， 需关
注欧盟在 10 月中旬中欧贸易磋商关键窗口期的政策表态是否升级贸易限制措施。

数据来源：海关总署、Wind，联合资信整理

二、内需：消费边际改善但结构分化延续，投资与房地产仍在寻底 
9 月，社会消费品零售总额同比增长 1.1%，较 8 月的 0.4%回升 0.7 个百分点。
剔除汽车以外的消费品零售额同比增长 3.9%，较 8 月的 2.5%明显改善，反映非汽车
类消费的修复动能在增强。 但汽车类仍是最大拖累：9 月汽车类零售额同比下降 21.2%，
降幅较 8 月的 18.5%进一步扩大。消费数据的边际改善部分源于低基数和以旧换新政
策的脉冲效应， 汽车等大宗消费的收缩尚未出现拐点， 商品消费的内生修复基础仍不
牢固。 
固定资产投资方面，1—9 月全国固定资产投资（不含农户）同比下降 7.2%，与
1—8 月降幅持平， 跌势未进一步加深， 但仍处于深度收缩区间。 分产业看， 第三产业
投资下降 10.0%， 仍是最大拖累； 第二产业投资下降2.7%， 其中制造业投资下降2.1%，
基础设施投资下降 4.2%。从资金端看，截至 9 月末，新增专项债累计发行完成全年
4.4 万亿元额度的约 76.9%，较上年同期进度稍缓但仍维持较大规模。专项债发行与
投资增速回稳 之间的时滞，表明资金从财政体系到项目落地的传导效率仍是投资端
修复的关键瓶颈。 
房地产市场方面，1—9 月全国房地产开发投资同比下降 20.6%，降幅较 1—8 月
扩大 0.7 个百分点； 其中住宅投资下降20.5%。1—9 月新建商品房销售面积同比下降
13.2%，销售额同比下降 13.9%，投资端与销售端同步走弱，且销售额降幅大于面积
降幅， 意味着房价仍在下行， 以价换量未能有效扭转市场预期。 二手房交易网签面积
同比增长 10.7%，与新房销售的下滑形成分化， 部分需求正从新房市场向二手房转移，
但这并未改变房地产开发投资深度收缩的整体格局。房地产市场的筑底过程仍在延续，
投资端对固定资产投资的拖累短期内难以消退。

数据来源：国家统计局、Wind，联合资信整理

三、工业：增长加快但结构高度集中 
9 月规模以上工业增加值同比增长 5.5%，较 8 月的 5.2%加快 0.3 个百分点，环
比增长 0.61%。生产端的扩张在总量层面是明确的， 但拆开门类与行业结构， 增长的
来源高度集中，传统部门的下行压力并未消退。 
三大门类中， 制造业增加值增长6.0%， 显著高于整体增速； 采矿业仅增长1.3%，
电力、热力、燃气及水生产和供应业增长 5.6%。制造业内部的分化更为鲜明：计算
机、通信和其他电子设备制造业增长 16.4%，专用设备制造业增长 12.1%，均显著高
于整体工业增速。与之对照，有色金属冶炼和压延加工业下降 5.1%，煤炭开采和洗
选业下降 3.3%，非金属矿物制品业下降 2.4%，黑色金属冶炼仅微增 1.6%。41 个大
类行业中 31 个保持增长，增长的覆盖面并不窄，但增速的“高度”几乎完全由高技
术制造和设备制造撑起，原材料部门则在负增长区间继续收缩。 
产品产量数据进一步印证了这一结构特征。9 月汽车产量同比下降 5.7%， 但其中
新能源汽车增长 16.9%；集成电路产量增长 18.4%；工业机器人增长 18.9%。而 9 月
原油加工量下降 8.4%， 水泥下降5.3%， 钢材下降3.8%。与能源转型、 设备更新和出
口链相关的产品保持双位数增长， 与传统基建和房地产周期绑定的重化工业品则延续
收缩。 
一个值得警惕的信号来自产销衔接。9 月出口交货值同比增长 17.0%，外需对工
业产出的吸收仍在发挥重要作用。生产端的景气在很大程度上依赖高技术制造业的高
增长和出口链的拉动，而内需对工业品的消化能力并未同步跟上 。

数据来源：国家统计局、Wind，联合资信整理

www.lhratings.com  研究报告 5
四、价格：PPI 创年内新高，剪刀差重新扩大
9 月 CPI 同比上涨 0.9%， 涨幅较8 月的 0.8%扩大 0.1 个百分点； 环比上涨0.2%，
涨幅较上月回落 0.2 个百分点。扣除食品和能源价格的核心 CPI 同比上涨 1.0%，涨
幅与上月相同。CPI 同比涨幅的扩大主要由能源价格驱动。受国际油价高位运行影响，
国内汽油价格同比上涨 16.6%，涨幅较上月大幅扩大 7.3 个百分点，影响 CPI 同比上
涨约 0.47 个百分点。服务价格同比上涨 0.8%，涨幅与上月持平；但环比由上月上涨
0.1%转为下降 0.2%，主要受暑期结束影响，飞机票、宾馆住宿和旅行社收费价格分
别下降 8.7%、7.0%和 6.0%。 食品价格同比仍为负增长， 同比下降1.7%， 降幅较上月
扩大 0.3 个百分点，其中猪肉和鲜菜价格分别下降 10.0%和 8.1%。AI 相关高技术产
品价格对 CPI 的正向贡献进一步显性化： 数据存储设备、 平板电脑、 计算机和移动电

话机价格同比分别上涨 46.7%、23.6%、21.8%和 14.9%， 涨幅均有扩大， 合计影响CPI
同比上涨约 0.23 个百分点。
PPI 方面，9 月全国工业生产者出厂价格同比上涨 5.0%，涨幅较 8 月的 3.8%大
幅扩大 1.2 个百分点， 创年内新高； 环比上涨1.2%， 涨幅较上月扩大0.8 个百分点，
由 8 月的微涨转为明显上行。PPI 同比涨幅的显著扩大由输入性因素与国内产业因素
共振驱动。 输入性方面， 国际原油价格高位运行带动石油和天然气开采业价格同比上
涨 24.5%，石油煤炭及其他燃料加工业上涨 21.6%，化学原料和化学制品制造业上涨
12.1%。国内因素方面，煤炭开采和洗选业价格同比上涨 35.3%，有色金属矿采选业
和冶炼压延加工业均上涨 21.4%。 新动能相关行业价格延续上行， 计算机通信和其他
电子设备制造业同比上涨 5.8%，电气机械及器材制造业上涨 6.2%。上述 8 个主要行
业合计影响 PPI 同比上涨约 5.42 个百分点。
CPI 与 PPI 的同比剪刀差在 9 月重新扩大。8 月剪刀差为 3.0 个百分点，9 月扩

大至 4.1 个百分点。剪刀差扩大的方向与 8 月“有所收窄”的趋势相反，核心原因在
于 PPI 的上行动力远强于 CPI。 价格信号的传导仍然不畅， 上游涨价高度集中于能源、
煤炭和有色金属等资源品领域， 中下游传统制造业和消费品行业的价格传导依然有限。
能源和资源品价格的脉冲式上行拉大了工业品与消费品之间的价格裂口， 核心CPI 的
平稳表现则说明终端需求的真实修复仍不足以形成全面的通胀传导链条。

### Page 6

www.lhratings.com  研究报告 6

数据来源：国家统计局、Wind，联合资信整理 
图 4  CPI、PPI 同比增速 
五、金融：社融超预期回升，政府债支撑，私人需求待修复 
9 月金融数据呈现“新增社融超预期回升、信贷总量仍偏弱但结构边际改善”的
格局。9 月新增社会融资规模 3.55 万亿元， 环比大幅增加1.89 万亿元， 同比多增192
亿元， 表现好于市场预期； 社融存量同比增速为7.1%，较8 月末的 7.2%小幅回落 0.1
个百分点。 前三季度社融增量累计为27.46 万亿元， 比上年同期少2.63 万亿元， 其中
对实体经济发放的人民币贷款同比少增 3.04 万亿元，社融口径下信贷收缩仍是主要
拖累。 
信贷需求仍偏弱， 但季末冲量带动环比大幅改善。9 月人民币贷款增加 1.08 万亿
元，环比大幅增加 1.02 万亿元，同比少增 2100 亿元。从结构看，企业端改善较为明
显： 企 （事） 业单位贷款增加1.29 万亿元， 同比多增700 亿元。居民端则持续疲弱：
9 月新增住户贷款为零，同比大幅减少 3890 亿元。消费贴息政策扩围落地和重点城
市楼市活跃度提高对居民信贷形成一定拉动， 但商品消费恢复有限、 房地产市场延续
分化格局，居民加杠杆意愿仍受到明显约束。政府债券仍是社融的核心支撑项。9 月
政府债券净融资 1.53 万亿元，同比多增 3445 亿元，在当月社融中的占比达 43%。与
此同时，政府融资增速结束了连续 13 个月的下行，在 9 月转为上行，财政部已安排
使用地方政府债务结存限额 5500 亿元，四季度政府融资规模大概率高于去年同期，
政府融资的拐点基本得以确认。但企业债券表现不及预期。企业债券净融资当月减少
425 亿元，为年内罕见负增。自 2022 年以来，企业债券融资在 9 月表现较弱，但只
有 2024 年负增长，2026 年再次负增长，这一情况后续值得密切跟踪观察。 
M2 与 M1 增速双双回落，资金活化程度走弱。9 月末 M2 同比增长 7.2%，较 8
月回落 0.3 个百分点；M1 同比增长 3.2%，较8 月回落 0.9 个百分点。M2 增速下滑主
要源于贷款增速回落，信贷派生的货币减少；M2 当月新增 2.82 万亿元，同比少增

政策层面，9 月的核心变化是增量政策从“部署”转向“落地”。9 月 28 日国务
院常务会议明确推出增量政策， 次日央行即落地四项工具。 往后看， 四季度财政端的
地方债务结存限额动用构成增量政策的下一个着力点。但在私人部门信贷需求仍弱、
专项债向实物工作量转化尚在途中的背景下，政策加力的效果更可能体现为“托底”
而非“反转” ，其向实体需求的传导效率仍是判断全年经济走势的关键变量。

Greater China Technology Hardware  | Asia Pacific
Higher OE Estimates, Upgrade
Largan and Genius
Following our semi team's increase in optical engine (OE)
estimates, we raise our fiber array unit (FAU) estimates
accordingly and thus upgrade Largan to OW and Genius to EW.
Morgan Stanley Asia Limited+

What’s Changed
Genius Electronic Optical Co.
Ltd. (3406.TW) From To
Rating Underweight Equal-weight
Price Target NT$910.00 NT$1,070.00
Largan Precision (3008.TW) From To
Rating Equal-weight Overweight
Price Target NT$7,100.00 NT$9,040.00
Raising FAU estimates after upstream semi team's OE estimates changes: Our OE

shipment forecasts increase by 128%/52% to 17.73mn/73.87mn in 2027/28 (see full
report). As OE and FAU are 1:1, we raise our FAU volume forecasts accordingly, while
key other assumptions are unchanged, which resulted in an FAU TAM increase  from
US$778mn to US$1.773bn in 2027e and from US$4.86bn to US$7.387bn in 2028e.
This significant FAU TAM increase leads to the changes below.
We now value Largan at NT$9,040/share, or 36% upside potential: Our analysis

suggests that Largan's share price already embeds >30% global market share in FAU
before formal production starts in 2H27 or even 2028. But our revised assumptions
suggest further upside if Largan successfully ramps up production and gains
meaningful market share. To reflect 3Q earnings miss, we trim our intrinsic value
estimates from NT$3,000 to NT$2,800 per share for the company's traditional
business, while our TAM analysis derives a value of NT$6,240/share  for CPO/FAU

new business, bringing the price target to NT$9,040. Considering the stronger
growth potential in 2028, we upgrade the stock to OW from EW.
We now value Genius at NT$1,070/share, upgrade to EW: Similarly, we believe
Genius's share price is also affected by hopes for FAU components.  Using a similar
approach, we conclude that the aggregate value is NT$1,070/share (NT$600 for
existing business and NT$470/share for the FAU component business). We therefore

see a more balanced risk/reward and hence move to EW.
Key risks to our call: While upstream semi capacity and yield might have already
improved, we believe the yield of FAU coupling/matching will be a key overhang.
Any lower-than-expected yield could result in  shipment misses for FAU and OE. If
the yield is not ideal, key customers are less likely to place large orders despite
upstream capacity being ready. On the upside, earlier-than-expected mass

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
We trim Largan's traditional business value to NT$2,800/share but raise FAU
business to NT$6,240/share, from which we derive a new PT of NT$9,040.
We value Genius's traditional business at NT$600/share and FAU business at NT
$470/share, from which we derive a new PT of NT$1,070.
We are more positive on FAU's TAM but, we think FAU's coupling/matching could

be an overhang to our higher OE/FAU shipment estimates in 2027-28.

Executive Summary
Higher optical engine shipment assumptions as yields
improve
Our semi team has lifted their optical engine shipment assumptions following their latest
supply-chain checks. While PIC production remains a bottleneck, improving SoIC yields
and downstream assembly yields should support a faster OE ramp. They now expect OE
production to reach 2mn/59mn/148mn units in 2026/27/28, with actual OE shipments
reaching 0.39mn/17.73mn/73.87mn units, respectively. In particular, they assume the SoIC

yield improves from 50% in 2026 to 95% in 2027 onwards, while downstream assembly
yield rises from 20% in 2026 to 30%/50% in 2027/28. We believe this provides greater
confidence in the CPO volume ramp, although downstream assembly and testing remain
key gating factors for how quickly OE shipments can scale.

Largan: Larger CPO/FAU upside; upgrade to OW
Based on the semi team's assumption revision, we lift our CPO optical engine volume
assumptions for 2027-28e while keeping other assumptions unchanged:
1. We forecast CPO optical engine volume to increase from 0.39mn in 2026 to
17.73mn in 2027, with a further surge to 73.87mn in 2028. Consequently, FAU
(Fiber Array Units – vital optical interconnect components that align multiple

optical fibers with silicon chips, making them critical for Co-Packaged Optics)
volumes for CPO also grow from 0.39mn to 73.87mn in the same period;
2. We assume FAU ASP of US$100 in 2026-28 (average market level);
3. We forecast Largan's FAU market share to increase from 0% in 2026 to 5% in
2027 and 40% in 2028.
4. Assuming net margins of 10% in 2027 and 30% in 2028, we forecast FAU earnings
of US$9mn in 2027 and US$882mn in 2028.

If we value the FAU business at 35x 2028e P/E (industry peers are at 40-50x P/E, but we
use a more conservative multiple to reflect the first year of scalable production), this
implies an incremental market cap of US$30.862bn. Discounting this back to 2026 at a
10% rate, we derive a per share positive impact of NT$6,240.

3Q26 results miss; revising down traditional business estimates. Largan reported 3Q26

revenue of NT$15.65bn, down 11% YoY, with gross margin declining to 42.2% and net
income of NT$5.18bn, down 27% YoY. The weaker-than-expected gross margin, mainly due
to lower new-product yields and higher structural green-power costs, resulted in a
meaningful earnings miss. Together with softer smartphone order momentum, we lower
our 2026-28 revenue estimates by 5-6% and gross margin assumptions to
45.8%/44.5%/47.5%, resulting in 12%/16%/9% downward revisions to our 2026-28

earnings estimates, respectively. For more details, please refer to Earnings Estimate
Revision                  section.
We remain positive on Largan's CPO/FAU opportunity given the company's strong track
record in high-precision manufacturing relative to most Taiwan peers. Following our semi
team's higher optical engine shipment assumptions, we see a meaningfully larger FAU
addressable market in 2027-28. More recently, Largan indicated that its first FA project

has effectively passed customer verification and that it is expanding from FA into FAU,
including coupling and potential FAU-to-carrier assembly, with dedicated capacity
expected to support mass production from around mid-2027/2H27. While the current
share price already reflects some CPO/FAU expectations, our revised assumptions suggest
further upside if Largan successfully ramps production and gains meaningful market share.
We set our price target at NT$9,040, comprising NT$2,800 from the traditional business

and NT$6,240 from the CPO/FAU business.
Genius: Higher CPO/FAU optionality; upgrade to EW

Similar to Largan, following semi-team's higher assumptions, we also lift our CPO optical
engine volume assumptions for 2027-2028e while keeping other assumptions unchanged.
1. We apply the same FAU volume and ASP assumptions as we do for Largan.
2. We expect Genius to be involved in FAU components, rather than overall
solutions; therefore, we assume components TAM to be 15% of the FAU TAM.
3. We forecast Genius's market share to increase from 0% in 2026 to 5% in 2027 and

30% in 2028.
4. Assuming net margins of 10% in 2027 and 25% in 2028, we forecast FAU
component earnings of US$1mn in 2027 and US$81mn in 2028.
If we value the FAU component business at 25x 2028e P/E (lower multiple than Largan
because Genius has smaller scale), it could introduce incremental market cap of US
$2,004mn. Discounting it back to 2026 at a 10% discounted rate, we derive a per-share
positive impact of NT$470.

Similar to Largan, our residual income model derives an intrinsic value of NT$600/share
for the traditional business, while the TAM analysis suggests CPO/FAU-related
opportunities could be valued at NT$470/share. Adding these together, we raise our price
target materially to NT$1,070.
Given Largan's traditional business has faced margin headwinds in 3Q26 due to the
application of green power, it's reasonable to expect that Genius could also face similar

customer requirements on green power. However, as Genius has more production capacity
in mainland China, we believe it could face less significant cost pressure. We therefore
maintain our estimates unchanged at this stage and will monitor margin development in
the upcoming quarterly results.

Valuation and Price Target Revisions
Largan: For the traditional business, we lower our earnings and medium-term growth rate;
as a result, our intrinsic value in our base case scenario is adjusted down to NT$2,800
from NT$3,000. We use RIM valuation methodology for Greater China Hardware Tech
companies in our coverage. We apply an unchanged CoE of 8.5%, a medium-term growth
rate of 8% (from 7% previously on higher 2026e estimates), and a long-term growth rate

of 3% (unchanged). However, we believe the upward revision to optical engine shipment
assumptions materially increases the addressable market and earnings potential for
Largan's FAU business. Therefore, we add the base case per share positive value from FAU
based on the analysis in the previous section. The new SOTP valuation derives a new price
target at NT$9,040 (from NT$7,100). We also raise our bull case and bear case values to
NT$11,426 (from NT$11,142.80)  and NT$4,620 (from NT$3,649.20).

Genius: For the traditional business, we make no earnings and intrinsic value revisions; as
a result, our intrinsic value under our base case scenario is unchanged at NT$600. We use
RIM valuation methodology for Greater China Hardware Tech companies in our coverage.
We apply a CoE of 8.5%, a medium-term growth rate of 4%, and a long-term growth rate
of 3%. However, we believe the upward revision to optical engine shipment assumptions
increases the potential value of Genius's FAU opportunity, although execution visibility

remains lower than for more established suppliers. Therefore, we add the base case per
share positive value from FAU based on the analysis in the previous section. The new
SOTP valuation derives a new price target at NT$1,070 (from NT$910). We also raise our
bull case and bear case values to NT$1,834 (from NT$1,705.20) and NT$658 (from NT
$592.40).

see a more balanced risk/reward and hence
we are EW.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

## Document 112: 261015_daiwa_TSMC

Source PDF: all report pdf/261015_daiwa_TSMC.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: TSMC continued delivering “beat-n-raise” prints for its 
quarterly results and guidance on 15 October. While we raise our forecasts 
to factor in its better topline and pricing trajectory yet muted margin 
expectation on an incremental basis, we see likely lack of near-term 
passion from investors on limited positive surprises from its results call this 
time. That said, we believe AI demand remains strong and sustainable to 
support our Buy (1) call on the stock, though patience may be necessary 
for investors amid the current jittery environment.

What’s the impact: Results intact, guidance mixed. Despite 3Q26 
topline of USD46.7bn topping guidance (USD44.6-45.8bn) at TSMC, its 
3Q26 EPS came in at TWD29.4, only marginally beating our/Bloomberg 
consensus estimates on lower opex and higher non-operating gains from 
long-term investments more than offsetting lower gross margin associated 
with higher depreciation costs on dilutive N2 ramp (table on p.2). Guidance 
calls for higher topline for 4Q26 with midpoint of the range 6% above our 
previous forecast, thanks to continued AI strength as customers continue to 
increase demand. Yet margins at the operating level looked muted without 
further operating leverage being expected by the street, due to the margin-
dilutive N2 ramp. We raise our 2026-28E forecasts by 3-10% to factor in 
the better topline trajectory, plus our view of better pricing power.

Lack of near-term passion? Apart from the upbeat topline but muted 
margin guidance on an incremental basis, we left the results call with 
limited positive surprises this time which may not drive any passion for 
investors in the near term to catalyse the stock. That said, fundamentals 
remain strong since AI demand continues to mushroom from accelerators 
to CPU and other companion chips, as language models transform from 
generative to agentic for AI assistance. TSMC implied upside risk to its 
long-term AI accelerator revenue CAGR of mid-to-high-50% over 2024-29 
and, as flagged in our Asian Foundry report, we saw upside risk to TSMC’s 
pricing power for 2027, given the still meaningful supply constraint where 
management reiterated its strong conviction in this multi-year AI megatrend 
and active capacity build aimed to narrow the supply/demand gap.

What we recommend: We raise our 12M TP for TSMC to TWD3,200 
(previous: TWD3,000), based on our finetuned ROE-adjusted PBR target of 
7.5x one-year forward BVPS forecast (previous: 7.7x). We reiterate our Buy 
(1) call with key downside risks of “silicon inflation” and geopolitical 
overhangs leading to global inflation and AI capex cut.

How we differ: Our 2028E EPS is 11% below the Bloomberg consensus 
likely on our view calling for a soft-landing in the chip cycle.

15 October 2026

Lack of passion despite strong AI?

➢ Another “beat-n-raise” in quarterly prints but likely … 
➢ … short of catalysts on limited positive surprise this time 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) call with a higher 12M TP of TWD3,200

Company profile 
Incorporated in Taiwan in 1987, Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Co. (TSMC) is the world’s largest 
complementary metal oxide semiconductor (CMOS) foundry in revenue terms. TSMC offers foundry 
services such as wafer masking, fabrication, probing, packaging and testing, to a high variety of 
customers including fabless chipmakers and IDMs. Its manufacturing fabs are located in Taiwan, China, 
the US and Singapore.

### Page 5

5

Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing (2330 TT): 15 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 15 Oct 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
TSMC's board of directors (BoD) has 10 members, including 6 independent directors
representing 60% of total directors. The background of TSMC's BoD is diversified, with members
from industries, academia, and law. Also, its board members include representatives from not
only Taiwan but also Europe and the US, bringing their experience in operating international
businesses to the board.
We believe the high percentage of independent directors and a diverse mix of expertise from the
board members strengthen the functionality of its BoD, as evidenced by TSMC's leading position
in the foundry market.
Capital management 1
TSMC pays its dividend on a quarterly basis. In the past 5 years, it has managed its dividend
payout well, as evidenced by its dividend in dollar terms, where it has never lowered its dividend.
As the leader in advanced technology nodes, TSMC has kept expanding its capacity for
advanced nodes; as such, we expect its capital intensity to increase to the level near or above
40% over 2025-26 (vs. its long-term target of mid-30%). However, we believe TSMC will at least
sustain its cash dividend payout in dollar terms over 2025-26E. We see its balance between
investment and dividend as appropriate.
Related party &
transaction 1
TSMC's sales to related parties and purchases from related parties remain immaterial as a
percentage of total revenue and cost of goods sold in 2026. We view these levels as insignificant
and therefore see limited risk to TSMC's earnings, cash flow generation, or corporate
governance.
S Supply chainmanagement 1
As the leader in the global foundry industry, TSMC approaches supply chain management by
focusing on 2 main strategies: sustainability risk management and local supply chain
optimisation. It requires all its suppliers to comply with its "Code with Ethics and Business
Conduct", and to follow regulations on human rights and conflict-free minerals.
In 2019, 100% of its tier-1 suppliers signed the "Supplier Code of Conduct" and self-assessment
questionnaire on sustainable management. Also, TSMC's raw materials purchased are 100%
Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) conflict-free. It also requires its critical suppliers to
report on the status of sustainability management in their critical upstream supply chains.
Through these measures, we see TSMC reducing its supply chain risk.
E Water & wastewatermanagement 2
TSMC applies internal and external company resources to develop water reclaimation
technology, implements water conservation measures and uses reeclaimed water in the
manufacturing process. For example, on September 19, 2022, TSMC Tainan Science Park
Reclaimed Water Plant started supplying reclaimed water. In 2025, TSMC aims to replace 5% of
water resources with reclaimed water, and continue to cooperate with the government to
complete the 2nd water reclamation plan in Anping, Tainan. In 2030, TSMC targets to replace >
60% of water resources with reclaimed water.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
TSMC's waste recycling rate reached 96% in 2022 for 8 years in a row. Also, the percentage of
waste sent to landfills was less than 1% for 13 years in a row in 2022.
TSMC achieved reducing its outsourced unit waste disposal (kg/12" equivalent wafer mask
layer) to 0.99 in 2022. Furthermore, TSMC has set a goal to reduce its outsourced unit waste
disposal per wafer to no more than 0.5 in 2030.
E GHG emissions 1
TSMC has continued its efforts to reduce greenhouse gas (GHG) emissions to drive low-carbon
manufacturing. In 2022, TSMC achieved net zero emissions of direct and indirect (from
purchased energy) GHG Emissions in overseas factories, and 100% use of
carbon-neutral natural gas in Taiwan fabs. It has set a target to achieve Net Zero Emissions by
2050.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

Greater China Technology Hardware  | Asia Pacific
Takeaways on Photonics from
2026 OCP Global Summit
Morgan Stanley Asia Limited+
Andy Meng, CFA

Andy.Meng@morganstanley.com                                                +852 2239-7689
Betty Chen
Research Associate
Betty.H.Chen@morganstanley.com                                                +852 2239-7213
Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line

On Day 3 of the 2026 OCP Global Summit, there is a full-day special focus on
Photonics. Industry participants such as NVIDIA, Meta, Microsoft, Oracle, Broadcom,
Celestica, Arista, Ciena, Amphenol, Lumentum, and Huawei presented their views.
We summarize the key findings below:
1) When the moderator asks key industry players whether they will apply CPO or
NPO, almost all companies answered 'Both'.
2) AI density depends on optical connectivity. With the day's focus on Photonics, the

importance of optical connectivity was well highlighted in each session.
3) Five key metrics for new optical technology: 1) performance, 2) power, 3) cost, 4)
speed to deployment and 5) reliability and serviceability. Huawei believes NPO has
advantages on maintainability, open decoupling, availability and industry
implementation.
4) Scale-in is another new approach to enhance AI density.
5) Root cause of CPO failures: 83-85% of failures due to ELS, 11-17% due to fiber/

connector. Dust and dirt are also common failure issues.
6) Optics vs. Copper: Full Optical Pod is targeted towards scale-up sizes >576
accelerators, Optics and Copper to coexist between 72 and 576 accelerators.
Greater China Technology Hardware: Takeaways on NPO from 2026 OCP Global
Summit (14 Oct 2026)

companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
A full day discussion of Photonics further demonstrated the importance of
optical connectivity.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -2.62 điểm tương ứng -0.15%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Dệt may, Công nghệ và Máy móc là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất trong 
tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -13,987 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã HDB, PNJ và VJC; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng ở mức 
-30 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều mua ròng 1,267 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua nhưng đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -2,363 hợp đồng. Tự doanh mua ròng 3,726 hợp 
đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -2,702 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam trải qua một tuần đi ngang giằng co, giảm nhẹ và đóng cửa ở 1.735 điểm 
với thanh khoản ở mức thấp, và nhà đầu tư nước ngoài bán ròng cả 5 phiên trong tuần. 
Thông tin vĩ mô tích cực với tăng trưởng GDP quý 3 đạt mức gần 10% là điểm sáng trong 
tuần qua, tuy đã không đủ nâng đỡ thị trường chung. Thay vào đó, tâm lý thị trường thận 
trọng trước số liệu lạm phát ở mức cao, đợt tăng giá xăng dầu trong nước và mặt bằng lãi 
suất trong nước chưa có dấu hiệu hạ nhiệt. Diễn biến tăng của giá dầu thế giới, và lợi suất 
trái phiếu Chính phủ Mỹ cũng là các yếu tố gây áp lực lên thị trường chung. Tâm lý thận 
trọng còn thể hiện qua áp lực bán giải chấp ở một số cổ phiếu riêng lẻ như PNJ, SHB..., dù 
một vài nhóm trụ như dầu khí, tiêu dùng có thời điểm đóng vai trò nâng đỡ chỉ số.

Trần Đức Anh 
Giám đốc Kinh tế Vĩ mô & Chiến lược thị trường 
anhtd@kbsec.com.vn 
07/10/2026

Triển vọng TTCK 4Q2026 
Tích lũy trong vùng nhiễu động

Chúng tôi dự báo vùng điểm hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm 2026 
xuống 1,800 đi ểm, giảm mạnh từ mức 1,950 đi ểm đưa ra trong báo cáo gần nhất. 
Vùng điểm này tương ứng:

- Tăng trưởng EPS 2026 so với cùng kỳ của thị trường chung ở mức 33% 
(tăng mạnh so với mức 25% của báo cáo gần nhất), chủ yếu nhờ kỳ vọng 
vào các chính sách thúc đẩy kinh tế của Chính phủ, và lợi nhuận tăng 
trưởng đột biến ở VIC (xem thêm phần Triển vọng hoạt động kinh doanh).

- Mức P/E hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm ở mức 13.2 (giảm 
nhẹ so với mức 13.5 thời điểm hiện tại - phản ánh quan điểm các yếu tố 
rủi ro sẽ tiếp tục duy trì). Đây là mức định giá thấp so với bình quân 5 năm 
gần nhất ở 15.1. Nếu loại bỏ yếu tố đột biến ở nhóm Vingroup, chỉ số P/E 
điều chỉnh hiện đã rơi xuống 10.9, thấp hơn các đáy gần nhất năm 2020, 
2022 và 2025. Trong bối cảnh kinh tế Việt Nam duy trì tốc độ tăng trưởng 
cao, lợi nhuận các doanh nghiệp niêm yết liên tục bứt phá, vùng định giá 
này mở ra cơ hội đầu tư trung/dài hạn. Chúng tôi cho rằng ngay khi các rủi 
ro vĩ mô quốc tế (xoay quanh vấn đề Iran), và trong nước (xoay quanh vấn 
đề lãi suất) hạ nhiệt, thị trường chung sẽ có cơ hội tái định giá lên mức cao 
hơn đáng kể so với thời điểm hiện tại. Dù vậy, khác với báo cáo gần nhất 
vào tháng 7, ở báo cáo này, chúng tôi đẩy lui thời điểm các yếu tố rủi ro vĩ 
mô hạ nhiệt sang năm 2027, đồng nghĩa với quan điểm thận trọng hơn về 
thị trường cho quý 4 2026.

Cụ thể, đối với vấn đề lãi suất, áp lực lạm phát lớn dần, đi kèm nhu cầu vốn cao phục 
vụ hoạt động đầu tư cơ sở hạ tầng sẽ khiến mặt bằng lãi suất duy trì ở mức cao cho 
phần còn lại của năm 2026 (dù thanh khoản hệ thống ghi nhận sự cải thiện đáng kể 
trong 1 quý gần đây). Đối với vấn đề xung đột Trung Đông, các cuộc đàm phán vẫn 
đang bế tắc và ít có kh ả năng dòng ch ảy dầu mỏ tại khu vực này được nối lại ngay 
trong năm nay.

Chủ đề đầu tư chúng tôi l ựa chọn cho 4Q2026 là đ ầu tư công, chính sách ti ền tệ hỗ 
trợ, và xung đột Iran.

Xem đầy đủ tại đây.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành mẫu nến Doji trên khung đồ thị tuần sau hai tuần giảm điểm liên tiếp, 
cho thấy áp lực bán đã phần nào được kiểm soát. Trên khung ngày, nỗ lực hồi phục đầu 
tuần gặp lực cản tại đường SMA50 khiến VNINDEX quay đầu giảm điểm, tiếp tục chớm phá 
đáy ngắn hạn; tuy nhiên vùng hỗ trợ gần đã tạo hiệu ứng thu hút lực cầu bắt đáy, giúp chỉ 
số thu hẹp đà giảm trong phiên cuối tuần. Sau nhịp điều chỉnh khá tiêu cực từ vùng đỉnh 
đầu tháng 9, VNINDEX đang bước vào giai đoạn tìm kiếm điểm cân bằng trở lại và diễn biến 
giằng co điều chỉnh có thể còn kéo dài thêm trong các phiên kế tiếp. Tuy nhiên khi xu 
hướng tăng ngắn hạn tính từ vùng đáy cuối tháng 7 vẫn đang được bảo lưu, chỉ số được kỳ 
vọng sẽ có cơ hội hồi phục trở lại tại điểm đỡ gần quanh 1710 (đây cũng là chốt chặn cần 
giữ để bảo vệ được xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn). 
Kháng cự xa: 1820 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1775 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1710 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1670 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Stronger Growth, Costlier Money
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
The latest GDP report and annual revisions show a sturdier U.S. economy than previously
reported. According to the Bureau of Economic Analysis (BEA), real GDP grew at a 2.2%
annualized pace in Q2 compared to 1.5% previously estimated, and Q1 was revised
from 2.1% to 2.5%. Both 2024 and 2025 were also revised slightly higher. Most of the

first half of the year revision was in categories related to the Artificial Intelligence (AI)
buildout, but real consumer spending has also been revised slightly higher, accelerating
from 0.7% annualized growth in Q1 to 3.8% in Q2 despite surging energy prices and
slow hiring.
Spending in July and August remained much stronger than labor market conditions alone
would suggest, also averaging 3.7% annualized growth. Upside revisions to net interest

income and personal saving suggest households have benefited from more income than
was previously apparent, with personal saving revised higher back to 2023. The new data,
according to the BEA, show the January to July 2026 savings rate averaged 4.8% versus
3.3% prerevision, strengthening consumer spending underpinnings despite moderate
hiring, high energy prices and rising borrowing costs.
The BEA update also included inflation methodological changes related to categories

inflated by the AI boom—such as portfolio management fees and computer software and
accessories. These improved the inflation picture to some extent—August “core” PCE
inflation was reduced by 0.3 ppts to 3% YoY, bringing it closer to “core” consumer price
index inflation, which was 2.5% YoY and 2% on a three-month annualized basis. While still
above the 2% Federal Reserve (Fed) target, “core” PCE now appears stable at 3% year over
year since January rather than accelerating. These methodological changes are expected

to dampen pass-through of AI-related price pressures into “core” inflation and effects of
stock-market swings on inflation.
They also produced an encouraging improvement in recent PCE inflation momentum.
Three-month annualized “core” PCE inflation slowed from 4.3% at the peak of tariff
effects in February to 2.1% in August. For durable goods, this rate was down from 5.8%
in February to 1.5% in August, and, for services ex-energy and healthcare, it slowed from

4.2% to 2.2%, for example. Inflation is volatile on a three-month change basis, so it
remains to be seen whether this moderation continues without further Fed tightening.
For now, the trends in the San Francisco Fed’s cyclical versus acyclical inflation measures
diverged again in August. As expected, given softening wage growth, prices most sensitive
to the labor market have eased further. At 3% YoY, this inflation category is down from
8% in early 2023 and 4.6% two years ago is back to prepandemic levels and is likely

to ease more (Exhibit 1A). In contrast, supply bottlenecks, high energy prices and other
industry-specific factors have kept acyclical inflation on an uptrend. As shown in Exhibit
1B, the relentless increase in freight costs could prolong upside pressure on this category.
The diesel market has tightened in recent months, boosting its price by 70% YoY,
consistent with Brent oil at around $200 per barrel and large reverberations across
a wide swath of goods and services prices. A government-policy-related decline in

available truck drivers is also raising transportation costs more than expected. So are
tight ocean shipping capacity and longer/more expensive voyages due to the U.S./Iran
War. Supply-chain stress also increased as companies rushed to front-run potential new
tariff increases.
On the positive side for the inflation outlook, unit labor costs (ULC) are up modestly
as robust productivity growth has absorbed much of the wage growth, limiting a major
source of domestic inflation. Upside productivity growth revisions are likely to further

reduce ULC, which could help shift focus from energy and AI-related inflation to
eventual disinflationary effects once oil and refined fuel prices start to ease. Sustained
productivity gains would support growth and margins while limiting inflation and the
need for much higher interest rates.
Investment Implications
We favor selective Equity exposure
to companies with durable demand
and pricing power, while remaining
cautious about businesses most

exposed to fuel, freight, and
financing costs. We would also
gradually increase long-duration
exposure.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
Surging nominal growth has boosted corporate revenues (up 13% YoY in Q2, according to the
BEA) while contained unit labor costs helped raise margins and profits more than expected.
With labor costs declining as a share of revenues, investment-related tax incentives and
rock-bottom net interest expense, pre-tax nonfinancial corporate profits as measured in GDP
accounts rose 21% YoY in Q2. Total corporate profits after-tax were up 18.2% YoY, according to

the BEA.
Surging AI-related imports and investment are keeping the business and profits cycle on
an uptrend both here and abroad. According to BofA Global Research, the global economic
cycle backdrop improved further in September, as reflected in a broad-based increase in its
Global Wave1 indicator. Broadening U.S. manufacturing strength, fueled by ongoing AI-driven
investment, a healthier orders backlog and low inventories suggest that the expansion has

room to run—a constructive environment for earnings. Typically, a Global Wave upswing is
consistent with rising earnings revisions ratios and equity price uptrends. According to the
Global Wave, global earnings growth expectations remain exceptionally strong.
Still, absent a resolution of war-related energy and transportation disruptions, the same
industrial strength intensifies inflation pressures. A prolonged supply shock raises the risk of
restrictive interest rates and weakening economic growth. In this context, the surge in the

10-year Treasury yield above 5% is starting to reflect more than optimism about U.S. growth
and strengthening productivity outlook. Oil’s tightest recent correlation with 10-year Treasury
yields in 35 years suggests a common energy-price thread behind rising yields in developed
countries.
Bond market spillovers from normalizing Japan yields and capital repatriation are another
common thread. Higher yields on foreign sovereign bonds such as in Japan—which has

escaped stagnation and deflation by intensely pursuing investment-led growth—weaken the
relative appeal of Treasurys. That distinction matters for portfolios. A yield increase driven
mainly by durable productivity gains would come with a stronger outlook for future income
and earnings. A yield increase driven by cost shocks and greater uncertainty raises financing
costs without the same benefit.
In sum, the economy remains more resilient than expected, but the bond market is also

charging more for an uncertain inflation outlook given untethered government debt and
cooling global demand for Treasurys spurred by geopolitical tensions and Japan capital
repatriation. The case for much further tightening becomes stronger if wage growth
strengthens, productivity lags, and unit labor costs reaccelerate, causing cyclical inflation to
also pick up steam. The case becomes weaker if that measure stays contained while fuel and
freight pressures recede or rising Treasury yields reduce asset prices and economic growth.

4 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
Yields, Yen and Iran: What Haunts the “House”
Lauren Sanfilippo, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
We recently highlighted the three-front challenge facing Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent:
engineering a stronger yen, containing the 10-year Treasury yield and securing a diplomatic
resolution to the conflict with Iran.

Bessent recently has delivered one of the year’s more memorable lines while discussing
Treasury policy and the yen: “I have asymmetric information. I am the house now.” 2 Yet several
challenges remain: the 10-year Treasury yield is near levels last seen in the early 2000s, the
yen remains structurally weaker, and a phased agreement to reopen the Strait of Hormuz
appears remote. The resulting strain on energy markets keeps inflation risks elevated.

Put differently, oil prices, Treasury yields and the dollar-yen exchange rate are all trending
against Bessent’s objectives. Below, we examine how each variable is haunting his “house.”
Haunting the “House” is the Yen. Since the U.S. and Japan mounted a record $98.7B
coordinated intervention in late July after the yen weakened to a 40-year low near 164 per
dollar, the currency has appreciated roughly 4% against the dollar. The move was further
supported on September 18 when the BoJ raised rates by 25 basis points (bps) to 1.25%,

their highest level since the mid-1990s (Exhibit 2A). However, the split 7–2 vote and Governor
Ueda’s limited forward guidance leave the path of further tightening uncertain. The key
question is whether additional BoJ hikes can produce sustained appreciation in the yen, or
merely reinforce the temporary relief provided by the summer intervention.
Bessent, meanwhile, has repeatedly signaled his preference for a stronger yen. Partly because
yen stability is also a U.S. financing concern. Persistent currency weakness could prompt

Japan to support its currency by selling foreign assets, including U.S. Treasurys—which would
be an unwelcome prospect as Washington partially relies on foreign capital flows to fund its
large fiscal deficits. That potential “haunts” the Treasury Secretary.  At precisely the moment
Washington needs foreign capital to finance its enormous fiscal deficits, it would be inopportune
for one of America’s largest creditors to become a significant seller of Treasury securities.

Haunting the “House” are Yields. The surge in yields runs counter to a White House intent
on lowering borrowing costs. The benchmark 10-year yield has broken above the 5% threshold
and climbed to levels not seen since 2007. At around 5.30%, it stands more than a full
percentage point above where it began the year (Exhibit 2B). The level is striking but so is the
speed of the move: Stronger-than-expected economic data, including a survey showing U.S.

2 September 8, 2026.
Investment Implications
While some key variables are
complicating the macro backdrop
(i.e., higher-for-longer yields/oil)
the combination of strong earnings
growth, robust consumer demand
(real final sales to private domestic
purchasers grew at 4.6% for Q2) and
ongoing capital investment suggests
that the fundamental backdrop for
U.S. Equities remains constructive.

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
business activity expanded at the fastest pace in more than five years, helped trigger an 18
bps surge in the 10-year yield—among its largest daily moves in decades. The 5-year yield also
topped 5% for the first time since 2007, while the 30-year yield reached 5.63%, its highest
level since 2002.
Compounding the move is inflation, running above the Fed’s 2% target for 67 months,

large fiscal deficits and the prospect of continued growth in Treasury issuance. Against this
backdrop, Treasury bought back $4.1 billion of long-dated debt, versus a maximum of $6
billion. That effort has done little to arrest the selloff. A subsequent auction priced nearly $4
billion of 7-year notes at 5.085%, the highest yield for that maturity in 33 years.
All in, the MOVE index, a key gauge of Treasury market volatility, jumped from 78 to 95 on

Wednesday September 23 and then to 104 the following day, its highest level since the early
days of the Iran war (Exhibit 3A).
Haunting the “House” is the Iran conflict. Early expectations that military pressure and
economic sanctions would quickly bring Iran to the negotiating table have yet to materialize.
Iranian officials have signaled that a full reopening of the Strait of Hormuz remains unlikely
while sanctions stay in place. Although talks between Washington and Tehran are continuing,

conflicting signals and lingering skepticism from both sides have kept tensions elevated.
As a result, oil prices have remained elevated in a range even as some Middle East supply
disruptions began to ease.
Startling is that Brent crude is up more than 60% this year, but refined product prices
have risen even faster. Ongoing disruptions to energy flows through the Strait of Hormuz,
combined with Ukrainian strikes on Russian refining infrastructure have tightened global

supplies of crude oil and refined fuels. Saudi Arabia has restored some drone-disrupted flows,
providing roughly 3.5 million barrels a day of relief (roughly 3% of global consumption).
The squeeze is mostly acute in diesel markets, where inventories remain constrained and
prices continue to climb (Exhibit 3B). U.S. diesel prices recently surpassed $6.50 per gallon,
the highest level on record, just as Russia has extended restrictions on diesel exports into

October. Against this backdrop, proposals to ban U.S. fuel exports risks worsening the
imbalance. According to S&P Global, an export ban could reduce refinery runs by nearly 2
million barrels per day and significantly cut gasoline production, potentially driving gasoline
and jet fuel prices even higher. As long as tensions persist, the oil impulse remains a key
impediment to structurally lower inflation.
Investment implications. Treasury Secretary Bessent increasingly sits at the intersection of

monetary policy, fiscal policy, foreign policy and national security. We remain constructive on
the U.S. economy and overweight U.S. Equities but are watching as these fronts challenge the
narrative. There is a great deal riding on the “house” versus the key macro variables of today.

6 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
When It Comes to Attracting Foreign Capital, the U.S. Remains Exceptional
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
There may be no better vote of confidence in a country than the willingness of
foreigners to put their money there. And by that measurement, the U.S. remains the
exception—both portfolio and FDI inflows are currently running at record highs based

on the latest data. This dynamic is one key reason why we remain constructive on U.S.
assets over the longer term.
Notwithstanding all the handwringing about America’s fiscal deficits, tariffs and rising
government debt, global investors sunk some $942B in net purchases of U.S. Equities in
the 12 months to July, the highest rolling 12-month total on record. In Q2, net purchase
of U.S. Equities soared to $426B, up 62% from the same period a year ago (Exhibit 4A).

By country, flows were down from China—not surprisingly—but strong from Taiwan and
South Korea, who have taken to recycling their massive AI-generated trade surplus back
into the U.S. In the end, strong portfolio inflows suggest that international investors
remain willing to increase their exposure to the world's largest capital market—that’s
bullish for U.S. assets.
Meanwhile, Exhibit 4B highlights the growing push among foreign multinationals to be

inside the U.S., with FDI inflows totaling nearly $240B in the first six months of this year.
That is a staggering 103% jump from a year ago and means FDI inflows are on track for
a record year, estimated at $480B for 2026.
There are multiple factors behind surging inflows: One, due to rising U.S. trade
protectionism, foreign multinationals increasingly want to be “inside” the U.S. economy,
not outside. Two, within the $32 trillion-plus economy with extraordinarily consumer

purchasing power, foreign firms prefer to be closer to their customers via investment
than just trade (exports). Three, America’s massive investment cycle in AI, data centers,
semiconductors, power generation, etc., has created numerous potential investment
opportunities for many foreign firms that don’t want to miss out on America’s surging
capex cycle. Four, and finally, abundant energy supplies, deep capital markets and a highly
developed technological ecosystem have also induced more FDI inflows.

In the end, double-barreled capital inflows speak to the absolute and relative
attractiveness of the U.S. economy and capital markets. Stay invested in America—the
rest of the world is.
Exhibit 4: U.S. Exceptionalism: Record Inflows of Portfolio and FDI Inflows.
A) Net Foreign Purchases of US Equities and Investment Funds. B) FDI Inflows to the U.S.

Investment Implications
Foreign investors remain keen on
owning and being “inside” the U.S. That
is bullish for U.S. securities, both near-

term and long-term.
